yai pana ‘‘santānavasaina pavattamānānaṃ dhammānaṃ avicchaidaina daisantaraisu pātubhāvau diṭṭhau, yathā taṃ vīhiādiaviñ‍ñāṇakasantānai, aivaṃ saviñ‍ñāṇakasantānaipi avicchaidaina daisantaraisu pātubhāvaina bhavitabbaṃ| ayañ‍ca nayau sati antarābhavai yuj‍jati, na añ‍ñathā’’ti yuttiṃ vadanti| taina hi iddhimatau caitauvasippattassa cittānugatikaṃ kāyaṃ  adhiṭṭhahantassa khaṇaina brahmalaukatau idhūpasaṅkamanai itau vā brahmalaukagamanai yutti vattabbā| yadi sabbatthaiva avicchin‍nadaisai dhammānaṃ pavatti icchitā, yadipi siyā iddhimantānaṃ iddhivisayau acintaiyyauti| taṃ idhāpi samānaṃ ‘‘kammavipākau  acintaiyyau’’ti vacanatau| tasmā taṃ taisaṃ matimattamaiva| acintaiyyasabhāvā hi sabhāvadhammā, tai katthaci pac‍cayavasaina vicchin‍nadaisai pātubhavanti, katthaci avicchin‍nadaisai| tathā hi mukhaghausādīhi pac‍cayaihi añ‍ñasmiṃ daisai ādāsapabbatappadaisādikai paṭibimbapaṭighausādikaṃ pac‍cayuppan‍naṃ nibbattamānaṃ dissati, tasmā na sabbaṃ sabbattha upanaitabbanti ayamaittha saṅkhaipau| vitthārau pana paṭibimbassa udāharaṇabhāvasādhanādikau antarābhavakathāvicārau kathāvatthupakaraṇassa (kathā॰ 505; kathā॰ aṭṭha॰ 505) ṭīkāyaṃ gahaitabbau|

aparai pana ‘‘idhāti kāmabhavau, huranti arūpabhavau, ubhayamantaraināti rūpabhavau vuttau’’ti| añ‍ñai ‘‘idhāti ajjhattikāyatanāni, huranti bāhirāyatanāni, ubhayamantaraināti cittacaitasikā’’ti| ‘‘idhāti vā pac‍cayadhammā, huranti pac‍cayuppan‍nadhammā, ubhayamantaraināti paṇṇattidhammā  vuttā’’ti vadanti| taṃ sabbaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu natthi| aivaṃ tāva ‘‘diṭṭhai diṭṭhamattaṃ bhavissatī’’tiādinā diṭṭhādivasaina catudhā taibhūmakadhammā saṅgahaitabbā| tattha subhasukhanic‍caattaggāhaparivaj‍janamukhaina asubhadukkhānic‍cānattānupassanā dassitāti haiṭṭhimāhi visuddhīhi saddhiṃ saṅkhaipainaiva vipassanā kathitā| ‘‘tatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, na tainā’’ti iminā rāgādīnaṃ samucchaidassa adhippaitattā maggau| ‘‘tatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, na tatthā’’ti iminā phalaṃ| ‘‘naividhā’’tiādinā anupādisaisā parinibbānadhātu kathitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘atha khau bāhiyassa…pai॰… āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cī’’ti|

imāya saṃkhittapadāya daisanāya tāvadaivāti tasmiṃyaiva khaṇai, na kālantarai| anupādāyāti aggahaitvā| āsavaihīti ābhavaggaṃ āgautrabhuṃ savanatau pavattanatau cirapārivāsiyaṭṭhaina madirādiāsavasadisatāya ca ‘‘āsavā’’ti  laddhanāmaihi kāmarāgādīhi| vimuc‍cīti samucchaidavimuttiyā paṭippassaddhivimuttiyā ca vimuc‍ci nissaj‍ji| sau hi satthu dhammaṃ suṇantau aiva sīlāni saudhaitvā yathāladdhaṃ cittasamādhiṃ nissāya vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā khippābhiñ‍ñatāya tāvadaiva sabbāsavai khaipaitvā  saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| sau saṃsārasautaṃ chinditvā katavaṭṭapariyantau antimadaihadharau hutvā aikūnavīsatiyā pac‍cavaikkhaṇāsu pavattāsu dhammatāya caudiyamānau bhagavantaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| ‘‘paripuṇṇaṃ tai pattacīvara’’nti puṭṭhau ‘‘na paripuṇṇa’’nti āha| atha naṃ satthā ‘‘taina hi pattacīvaraṃ pariyaisā’’ti vatvā pak‍kāmi| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘atha khau bhagavā…pai॰… pak‍kāmī’’ti|

sau kira kassapadasabalassa sāsanai vīsavassasahassāni samaṇadhammaṃ karauntau ‘‘bhikkhunā nāma attanā pac‍cayai labhitvā yathādānaṃ karauntaina attanāva paribhuñ‍jituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti aikassa bhikkhussapi pattaina vā cīvaraina vā saṅgahaṃ nākāsi, tainassa aihibhikkhuupasampadāya upanissayau nāhausi| kaici panāhu – ‘‘sau kira buddhasuñ‍ñai laukai caurau hutvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā arañ‍ñai caurikaṃ karauntau aikaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ disvā pattacīvaralaubhaina taṃ usunā vijjhitvā pattacīvaraṃ gaṇhi, tainassa iddhimayapattacīvaraṃ na uppaj‍jissatīti, satthā taṃ ñatvā aihibhikkhubhāvaina pabbaj‍jaṃ na adāsī’’ti| tampi pattacīvarapariyaisanaṃ caramānaṃ aikā dhainu vaigaina āpatantī paharitvā jīvitakkhayaṃ pāpaisi| taṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘atha khau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau bāhiyaṃ dārucīriyaṃ gāvī taruṇavacchā adhipatitvā jīvitā vauraupaisī’’ti|

tattha  acirapak‍kantassāti na ciraṃ pak‍kantassa bhagavatau| gāvī taruṇavacchāti aikā yakkhinī taruṇavacchadhainurūpā| adhipatitvāti abhibhavitvā madditvā| jīvitā vauraupaisīti purimasmiṃ attabhāvai laddhāghātatāya diṭṭhamattainaiva  vairicittaṃ uppādaitvā siṅgaina paharitvā jīvitā vauraupaisi|

satthā piṇḍāya caritvā katabhattakic‍cau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ nagaratau nikkhamantau bāhiyassa sarīraṃ saṅkāraṭṭhānai patitaṃ disvā bhikkhū āṇāpaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavai, aikasmiṃ gharadvārai ṭhatvā mañ‍cakaṃ āharāpaitvā idaṃ sarīraṃ nagaratau nīharitvā jhāpaitvā thūpaṃ karauthā’’ti, bhikkhū tathā akaṃsu| katvā ca pana vihāraṃ gantvā satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā attanā katakic‍caṃ āraucaitvā tassa abhisamparāyaṃ pucchiṃsu| atha naisaṃ bhagavā tassa parinibbutabhāvaṃ ācikkhi| bhikkhū ‘‘tumhai, bhantai, ‘bāhiyau dārucīriyau arahattaṃ pattau’ti vadatha, kadā sau arahattaṃ pattau’’ti  pucchiṃsu| ‘‘mama dhammaṃ sutakālai’’ti ca vuttai ‘‘kadā panassa tumhaihi dhammau kathitau’’ti? ‘‘piṇḍāya carantaina aj‍jaiva antaravīthiyaṃ ṭhatvā’’ti| ‘‘appamattakau sau, bhantai, tumhaihi antaravīthiyaṃ ṭhatvā kathitadhammau, kathaṃ sau tāvatakaina visaisaṃ nibbattaisī’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ, bhikkhavai, mama dhammaṃ ‘appaṃ vā bahuṃ vā’ti pamiṇatha, anaikāni gāthāsahassānipi anatthasaṃhitāni na saiyyau, atthanissitaṃ pana aikampi gāthāpadaṃ saiyyau’’ti dassaintau –

‘‘sahassamapi cai gāthā, anatthapadasañhitā|

aikaṃ gāthāpadaṃ saiyyau, yaṃ sutvā upasammatī’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 101) –

dhammapadai imaṃ gāthaṃ vatvā ‘‘na kaivalaṃ sau parinibbānamattaina, atha khau mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñānaṃ aggabhāvainapi pūjārahau’’ti dassaintau ‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñānaṃ, yadidaṃ bāhiyau dārucīriyau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 1.216) taṃ āyasmantaṃ aitadaggai ṭhapaisi| taṃ  sandhāya vuttaṃ – ‘‘atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā…pai॰… parinibbutau, bhikkhavai, bāhiyau dārucīriyau’’ti|

tattha pacchābhattanti bhattakic‍catau pacchā| piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantauti piṇḍapātapariyaisanatau paṭinivattau| padadvayaināpi katabhattakic‍cauti vuttaṃ hauti| nīharitvāti nagaratau bahi naitvā| jhāpaithāti dahatha| thūpañ‍cassa karauthāti assa bāhiyassa sarīradhātuyau gahaitvā caitiyañ‍ca karautha| tattha kāraṇamāha – ‘‘sabrahmacārī vau, bhikkhavai, kālakatau’’ti| tassatthau – yaṃ tumhai saiṭṭhaṭṭhaina brahmaṃ adhisīlādipaṭipattidhammaṃ sandiṭṭhaṃ caratha, taṃ sau tumhaihi samānaṃ brahmaṃ acarīti sabrahmacārī maraṇakālassa pattiyāva kālakatau, tasmā taṃ mañ‍cakaina nīharitvā jhāpaitha, thūpañ‍cassa karauthāti|

tassa kā gatīti pañ‍casu gatīsu tassa katamā gati upapatti bhavabhūtā, gatīti nipphatti, ariyau puthuj‍janau vāti kā niṭṭhāti atthau| abhisamparāyauti paic‍ca bhavuppatti bhavaniraudhau vā| kiñ‍cāpi tassa thūpakaraṇāṇattiyāva parinibbutabhāvau atthatau pakāsitau hauti, yai pana bhikkhū tattakaina na jāniṃsu, tai ‘‘tassa kā gatī’’ti  pucchiṃsu| pākaṭataraṃ vā kārāpaitukāmā tathā bhagavantaṃ pucchiṃsu|

paṇḍitauti aggamaggapañ‍ñāya adhigatattā paṇḍaina itau gatau pavattauti paṇḍitau| pac‍capādīti paṭipaj‍ji| dhammassāti laukuttaradhammassa| anudhammanti sīlavisuddhiādipaṭipadādhammaṃ| atha vā dhammassāti nibbānadhammassa| anudhammanti ariyamaggaphaladhammaṃ| na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇanti dhammadaisanāhaitu na ca maṃ vihaisaisi yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipan‍nattā| yau hi satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ vā gahaitvā yathānusiṭṭhaṃ na paṭipaj‍jati, sau satthāraṃ vihaisaiti nāma| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ – ‘‘vihiṃsasañ‍ñī paguṇaṃ na bhāsiṃ, dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujaisu brahmai’’ti (mahāva॰ 9; ma॰ ni॰ 1.283; 2.339)| atha vā na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇanti na ca imaṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – vaṭṭadukkhatau niyyānahaitubhūtaṃ imaṃ mama sāsanadhammaṃ suppaṭipan‍nattā na vihaisaiti| duppaṭipan‍nau hi sāsanaṃ bhindantau satthu  dhammasarīrai pahāraṃ daiti nāma| ayaṃ pana sammāpaṭipattiṃ matthakaṃ pāpaitvā anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyi| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘parinibbutau, bhikkhavai, bāhiyau dārucīriyau’’ti|

aitamatthaṃ viditvāti aitaṃ thairassa bāhiyassa anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbutabhāvaṃ, tathā parinibbutānañ‍ca khīṇāsavānaṃ gatiyā pacurajanaihi dubbiñ‍ñaiyyabhāvaṃ sabbākāratau viditvā| imaṃ udānanti imaṃ appatiṭṭhitaparinibbānānubhāvadīpakaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

tattha yatthāti yasmiṃ nibbānai āpau ca na gādhati, pathavī ca taijau ca vāyau ca na gādhati, na patiṭṭhāti| kasmā? nibbānassa asaṅkhatasabhāvattā| na hi tattha saṅkhatadhammānaṃ laisaupi  sambhavati| suk‍kāti suk‍kavaṇṇatāya suk‍kāti laddhanāmā gahanakkhattatārakā| na jautantīti na bhāsanti| ādic‍cau nappakāsatīti tīsu dīpaisu aikasmiṃ khaṇai ālaukapharaṇasamatthau ādic‍caupi ābhāvasaina na dibbati| na tattha candimā bhātīti satipi bhāsurabhāvai kantasītalakiraṇau candaupi tasmiṃ nibbānai abhāvatau aiva attanau juṇhāvibhāsanaina na viraucati| yadi tattha candimasūriyādayau natthi, laukantarau viya nic‍candhakāramaiva  taṃ bhavaiyyāti āsaṅkaṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘tamau tattha na vij‍jatī’’ti| sati hi rūpābhāvai tamau nāma na siyā|

yadā ca attanā vaidi, muni maunaina brāhmaṇauti catusac‍camunanatau maunanti laddhanāmaina maggañāṇaina kāyamaunaiyyādīhi ca saman‍nāgatattā ‘‘munī’’ti laddhanāmau ariyasāvakabrāhmaṇau tainaiva maunasaṅkhātaina paṭivaidhañāṇaina yadā yasmiṃ kālai aggamaggakkhaṇai attanā sayamaiva anussavādikai pahāya attapac‍cakkhaṃ katvā nibbānaṃ vaidi paṭivijjhi| ‘‘avaidī’’tipi pāṭhau, añ‍ñāsīti atthau| atha rūpā arūpā ca, sukhadukkhā pamuc‍catīti athāti tassa nibbānassa jānanatau pacchā| rūpāti rūpadhammā, taina pañ‍cavaukārabhavau aikavaukārabhavau ca gahitau hauti| arūpāti arūpadhammā, taina rūpaināmissīkatau  arūpabhavau gahitau hauti| sau ‘‘catuvaukārabhavau’’tipi vuc‍cati| sukhadukkhāti sabbattha uppaj‍janakasukhadukkhataupi vaṭṭatau| atha vā rūpāti rūpalaukapaṭisandhitau| arūpāti arūpalaukapaṭisandhitau| sukhadukkhāti kāmāvacarapaṭisandhitau| kāmabhavau hi byāmissasukhadukkhau| aivamaitasmā sakalataupi vaṭṭatau ac‍cantamaiva muc‍catīti gāthādvayainapi bhagavā ‘‘mayhaṃ puttassa bāhiyassa aivarūpā nibbānagatī’’ti dassaiti|

dasamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

niṭṭhitā ca baudhivaggavaṇṇanā|

2. mucalindavaggau

1. mucalindasuttavaṇṇanā

11. mucalindavaggassa  paṭhamai mucalindamūlaiti aittha mucalindau vuc‍cati nīparukkhau| sau ‘‘niculau’’tipi vuc‍cati, tassa samīpai| kaici pana ‘‘mucalauti tassa rukkhassa nāmaṃ, taṃ vanajaiṭṭhakatāya pana mucalindauti vutta’’nti vadanti| mahā akālamaighauti asampattai vassakālai uppan‍namahāmaighau| sau hi gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai sakalacak‍kavāḷagabbhaṃ pūraintau udapādi| sattāhavaddalikāti tasmiṃ uppan‍nai sattāhaṃ avicchin‍navuṭṭhikā ahausi| sītavātaduddinīti sā ca sattāhavaddalikā udakaphusitasammissaina sītavātaina samantatau paribbhamantaina dusitadivasattā duddinī nāma ahausi| mucalindau nāma nāgarājāti tassaiva mucalindarukkhassa samīpai paukkharaṇiyā haiṭṭhā nāgabhavanaṃ atthi, tattha nibbattau mahānubhāvau nāgarājā| sakabhavanāti attanau nāgabhavanatau| sattakkhattuṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvāti sattavārai attanau sarīrabhaugaihi bhagavatau kāyaṃ parivāraitvā| uparimuddhani mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ vihac‍cāti bhagavatau muddhappadaisassa upari attanau mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ pasāraitvā| ‘‘phaṇaṃ karitvā’’tipi pāṭhau, sau aivatthau|

tassa kira nāgarājassa aitadahausi ‘‘bhagavā ca mayhaṃ bhavanasamīpai rukkhamūlai nisin‍nau, ayañ‍ca sattāhavaddalikā vattati, vāsāgāramassa laddhuṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti| sau sattaratanamayaṃ pāsādaṃ nimminituṃ sak‍kauntaupi ‘‘aivaṃ katai kāyasārau gahitau na bhavissati, dasabalassa kāyavaiyyāvac‍caṃ karissāmī’’ti mahantaṃ attabhāvaṃ katvā  satthāraṃ sattakkhattuṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvā upari phaṇaṃ katvā dhāraisi| ‘‘parikkhaipabbhantaraṃ lauhapāsādai bhaṇḍāgāragabbhappamāṇaṃ ahausī’’ti khandhakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ (mahāva॰ aṭṭha॰ 5) vuttaṃ| majjhimaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘haiṭṭhālauhapāsādappamāṇa’’nti (ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.284)| ‘‘icchiticchitaina iriyāpathaina satthā viharissatī’’ti kira nāgarājassa ajjhāsayau| bhagavā pana yathānisin‍nauva sattāhaṃ vītināmaisi| tañ‍ca ṭhānaṃ supihitavātapānaṃ suphusitaaggaḷadvāraṃ kūṭāgāraṃ viya ahausi| mā  bhagavantaṃ sītantiādi tassa tathā karitvā ṭhānakāraṇaparidīpanaṃ| sau hi  ‘‘mā bhagavantaṃ sītaṃ bādhayittha, mā uṇhaṃ, mā ḍaṃsādisamphassau bādhayitthā’’ti tathā karitvā aṭṭhāsi|

tattha kiñ‍cāpi sattāhavaddalikāya uṇhamaiva natthi, sacai pana antarantarā maighau vigacchaiyya, uṇhaṃ bhavaiyya, tampi mā bādhayitthāti aivaṃ tassa cintaituṃ yuttaṃ| kaici panaittha vadanti ‘‘uṇhaggahaṇaṃ bhaugaparikkhaipassa vipulabhāvakaraṇai kāraṇakittanaṃ| khuddakai hi tasmiṃ bhagavantaṃ nāgassa sarīrasambhūtā usmā bādhaiyya, vipulabhāvakaraṇaina pana tādisaṃ ‘mā uṇhaṃ bādhayitthā’ti tathā karitvā aṭṭhāsī’’ti|

viddhanti ubbiddhaṃ, maighavigamaina dūrībhūtanti atthau| vigatavalāhakanti apagatamaighaṃ| daivanti ākāsaṃ| viditvāti ‘‘idāni vigatavalāhakau ākāsau, natthi bhagavatau sītādiupaddavau’’ti ñatvā| vinivaiṭhaitvāti apanaitvā| sakavaṇṇanti attanau nāgarūpaṃ| paṭisaṃharitvāti antaradhāpaitvā| māṇavakavaṇṇanti kumārakarūpaṃ|

aitamatthanti vivaikasukhappaṭisaṃvaidinau yattha katthaci sukhamaiva hautīti aitamatthaṃ sabbākāraina jānitvā| imaṃ udānanti imaṃ vivaikasukhānubhāvadīpakaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

tattha sukhau vivaikauti nibbānasaṅkhātau upadhivivaikau sukhau| tuṭṭhassāti catumaggañāṇasantausaina tuṭṭhassa| sutadhammassāti pakāsitadhammassa vissutadhammassa| passatauti taṃ vivaikaṃ, yaṃ vā kiñ‍ci passitabbaṃ nāma, taṃ sabbaṃ  attanau vīriyabalādhigataina ñāṇacakkhunā passantassa| abyāpaj‍janti akuppanabhāvau, aitaina maittāpubbabhāgau dassitau| pāṇabhūtaisu saṃyamauti sattaisu ca saṃyamau avihiṃsanabhāvau sukhauti atthau| aitaina karuṇāpubbabhāgau dassitau|

sukhā virāgatā laukaiti vigatarāgatāpi laukai sukhā| kīdisī? kāmānaṃ samatik‍kamauti, yā kāmānaṃ samatik‍kamauti vuc‍cati, sā vigatarāgatāpi sukhāti atthau, aitaina anāgāmimaggau kathitau| asmimānassa yau vinayauti iminā pana arahattaṃ kathitaṃ| arahattañhi asmimānassa paṭippassaddhivinayauti vuc‍cati, itau parañ‍ca sukhaṃ nāma natthi, taināha ‘‘aitaṃ vai paramaṃ sukha’’nti| aivaṃ arahattaina daisanāya kūṭaṃ gaṇhīti|

paṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. rājasuttavaṇṇanā

12. dutiyai  sambahulānanti vinayapariyāyaina tayau janā ‘‘sambahulā’’ti vuc‍canti, tatau paraṃ saṅghau| suttantapariyāyaina pana tayau tayau aiva, tatau uddhaṃ sambahulā| tasmā idhāpi suttantapariyāyaina sambahulāti vaiditabbā| upaṭṭhānasālāyanti dhammasabhāmaṇḍapai| sā hi dhammaṃ daisaituṃ āgatassa tathāgatassa bhikkhūnaṃ upaṭṭhānakaraṇaṭṭhānanti ‘‘upaṭṭhānasālā’’ti vuc‍cati| atha vā yattha bhikkhū vinayaṃ vinicchinanti, dhammaṃ kathainti, sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍janti, san‍nipatanavasaina pakatiyā upatiṭṭhanti, sā sālāpi maṇḍapaupi ‘‘upaṭṭhānasālā’’tvaiva vuc‍cati| tatthāpi hi buddhāsanaṃ nic‍caṃ pañ‍ñattamaiva hauti| idañhi buddhānaṃ dharamānakālai  bhikkhūnaṃ cārittaṃ| san‍nisin‍nānanti nisaj‍janavasaina saṅgamma nisin‍nānaṃ| san‍nipatitānanti tatau tatau āgantvā san‍nipatanavasaina san‍nipatitānaṃ| atha vā buddhāsanaṃ puratau katvā satthu sammukhai viya ādaruppattiyā sak‍kac‍caṃ nisīdanavasaina san‍nisin‍nānaṃ, samānajjhāsayattā añ‍ñamañ‍ñasmiṃ ajjhāsayaina suṭṭhu sammā ca nipatanavasaina san‍nipatitānaṃ| ayanti idāni vuc‍camānaṃ niddisati| antarākathāti kammaṭṭhānamanasikārauddaisaparipucchādīnaṃ antarā añ‍ñā aikā kathā, atha vā majjhanhikai laddhassa sugatauvādassa, sāyaṃ labhitabbassa dhammassavanassa ca antarā pavattattā antarākathā, samaṇasamācārassaiva vā antarā pavattā añ‍ñā aikā kathāti antarākathā| udapādīti uppan‍nā|

imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ rājūnanti niddhāraṇai sāmivacanaṃ| mahaddhanatarau vātiādīsu pathaviyaṃ nikhaṇitvā ṭhapitaṃ sattaratananicayasaṅkhātaṃ mahantaṃ dhanaṃ aitassāti mahaddhanau, dvīsu ayaṃ atisayaina mahaddhanauti mahaddhanatarau| vāsaddau vikappatthau| saisapadaisupi aisaiva nayau| ayaṃ pana visaisau – nic‍caparibbayavasaina mahantau bhaugau aitassāti mahābhaugau| daivasikaṃ pavisanaāyabhūtau mahantau kausau aitassāti mahākausau| aparai pana ‘‘daivasikaṃ pavisanaāyabhūtaṃ maṇisāraphaiggugumbādibhaidabhin‍naṃ pariggahavatthu dhanaṃ, tadaiva sāragabbhādīsu nihitaṃ kausau’’ti vadanti| vajirau, mahānīlau, indanīlau, marakatau, vaiḷuriyau, padumarāgau, phussarāgau, kak‍kaitanau, pulākau, vimalau, lauhitaṅkau, phalikau, pavāḷau, jautirasau, gaumuttakau, gaumaidakau, saugandhikau, muttā, saṅkhau, añ‍janamūlau, rājapaṭṭau, amataṃsakau, piyakau, brāhmaṇī cāti catubbīsati maṇi nāma| satta  lauhāni kahāpaṇau ca sārau nāma| sayanacchādanapāvuraṇagajadantasilādīni phaiggu nāma| candanāgarukuṅkumatagarakappūrādi gumbā nāma| tattha purimaina ādisaddaina sālivīhiādimuggamāsādipubbaṇṇāparaṇṇabhaidaṃ dhañ‍ñavikatiṃ ādiṃ  katvā yaṃ sattānaṃ upabhaugaparibhaugabhūtaṃ vatthu, taṃ sabbaṃ saṅgayhati| mahantaṃ vijitaṃ raṭṭhaṃ aitassāti mahāvijitau| mahantau hatthiassādivāhanau aitassāti mahāvāhanau| mahantaṃ sainābalañ‍caiva thāmabalañ‍ca aitassāti mahabbalau| icchitanibbattisaṅkhātā puñ‍ñakammanipphan‍nā mahatī iddhi aitassāti mahiddhikau| taijasaṅkhātau ussāhamantapabhusattisaṅkhātau vā mahantau ānubhāvau aitassāti mahānubhāvau|

aittha ca paṭhamaina āyasampadā, dutiyaina vittūpakaraṇasampadā, tatiyaina vibhavasampadā, catutthaina janapadasampadā, pañ‍camaina yānasampadā, chaṭṭhaina parivārasampadāya saddhiṃ attasampadā, sattamaina puñ‍ñakammasampadā, aṭṭhamaina pabhāvasampadā taisaṃ rājūnaṃ pakāsitā hauti| taina yā sā sāmisampatti, amac‍casampatti, saināsampatti, raṭṭhasampatti, vibhavasampatti, mittasampatti, duggasampattīti satta pakatisampadā rājūnaṃ icchitabbā| tā sabbā yathārahaṃ paridīpitāti vaiditabbā|

dānādīhi catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi parisaṃ rañ‍jaitīti rājā| magadhānaṃ issarauti māgadhau| mahatiyā saināya saman‍nāgatattā sainiyagauttattā vā sainiyau| bimbi vuc‍cati suvaṇṇaṃ, tasmā sārabimbivaṇṇatāya bimbisārau| kaici pana ‘‘nāmamaivaitaṃ tassa rañ‍ñau’’ti vadanti| pac‍cāmittaṃ parasainaṃ jinātīti pasainadi| kausalaraṭṭhassa adhipatīti kausalau| ayañ‍carahīti aittha carahīti nipātamattaṃ| vippakatāti apariyausitā| ayaṃ taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā aniṭṭhitāti  atthau|

sāyanhasamayanti sāyanhai aikaṃ samayaṃ| paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitauti tatau tatau rūpādiārammaṇatau cittassa paṭisaṃharaṇatau paṭisal‍lānasaṅkhātāya  phalasamāpattitau yathākālaparicchaidaṃ vuṭṭhitau| bhagavā hi pubbaṇhasamayaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau sāvatthiṃ pavisitvā bhikkhūnaṃ sulabhapiṇḍapātaṃ katvā katabhattakic‍cau bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ sāvatthitau nikkhamitvā vihāraṃ pavisitvā gandhakuṭippamukhai ṭhatvā vattaṃ dassaitvā ṭhitānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yathāsamuṭṭhitaṃ sugatauvādaṃ datvā taisu arañ‍ñarukkhamūlādidivāṭṭhānaṃ uddissa gataisu gandhakuṭiṃ pavisitvā phalasamāpattisukhaina divasabhāgaṃ vītināmaitvā yathākālaparicchaidai samāpattitau vuṭṭhāya, ‘‘mayhaṃ upagamanaṃ āgamayamānā catassau parisā sakalavihāraṃ paripūraintiyau nisin‍nā, idāni mai dhammadaisanatthaṃ dhammasabhāmaṇḍalaṃ upagantuṃ kālau’’ti āsanatau vuṭṭhāya, kaisarasīhau viya kañ‍canaguhāya surabhigandhakuṭitau nikkhamitvā yūthaṃ upasaṅkamantau mattavaravāraṇau viya akāyacāpal‍laina  cāruvik‍kantagamanau asītianubyañ‍janappaṭimaṇḍitabāttiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇasamuj‍jalāya byāmappabhāya parikkhaipavilāsasampan‍nāya pabhassarakaitumālālaṅkatāya nīlapītalauhitaudātamañ‍jiṭṭhapabhassarānaṃ vasaina chabbaṇṇabuddharaṃsiyau vissaj‍jaintiyā acintaiyyānubhāvāya anupamāya buddhalīlāya saman‍nāgatāya rūpakāyasampattiyā sakalavihāraṃ aikālaukaṃ kurumānau upaṭṭhānasālaṃ upasaṅkami| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘atha khau bhagavā…pai॰… tainupasaṅkamī’’ti|

aivaṃ upasaṅkamitvā vattaṃ dassaitvā nisin‍nai tai bhikkhū tuṇhībhūtai disvā ‘‘mayi akathaintai imai bhikkhū buddhagāravaina kappampi na kathaissantī’’ti kathāsamuṭṭhāpanatthaṃ ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai’’tiādimāha| tattha kāya nutthāti katamāya nu bhavatha| ‘‘kāya nautthā’’tipi pāḷi, sau aivatthau, ‘‘kāya nvaitthā’’tipi paṭhanti, tassa katamāya nu aitthāti atthau| tatrāyaṃ saṅkhaipatthau – bhikkhavai, katamāya  nāma kathāya idha san‍nisin‍nā bhavatha, katamā ca tumhākaṃ kathā mamāgamanapac‍cayā aniṭṭhitā, taṃ niṭṭhāpaissāmīti aivaṃ sabbañ‍ñupavāraṇāya pavāraisi|

na khvaitanti na khau aitaṃ, ayamaiva vā pāṭhau| ‘‘na khauta’’ntipi paṭhanti, na khau aitaṃ ic‍caiva padavibhāgau| kulaputtānanti jātiācārakulaputtānaṃ| saddhāti saddhāya, kammaphalasaddhāya ratanattayasaddhāya ca| agārasmāti gharatau, gahaṭṭhabhāvāti atthau| anagāriyanti pabbaj‍jaṃ| pabbajitānanti upagatānaṃ| yanti kiriyāparāmasanaṃ| tatthāyaṃ padayaujanā – ‘‘bhikkhavai, tumhai naiva rājābhinītā na caurābhinītā na iṇaṭṭā na jīvitapakatā pabbajitā, atha khau saddhāya agāratau nikkhamitvā mama sāsanai pabbajitā, tumhai aitarahi aivarūpiṃ rājappaṭisaṃyuttaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaiyyātha, yaṃ aivarūpāya kathāya kathanaṃ, aitaṃ tumhākaṃ na khau patirūpaṃ na yuttamaivā’’ti|

aivaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ pabbajitānaṃ appatirūpaṃ paṭikkhipitvā idāni naisaṃ patirūpaṃ paṭipattiṃ anujānantau ‘‘san‍nipatitānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ dhammī vā kathā ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau’’ti āha| tattha vauti tumhākaṃ| karaṇīyanti hi padaṃ apaikkhitvā kattari sāmivacanamaitaṃ, tasmā tumhaihīti atthau| dvayaṃ karaṇīyanti dvai kātabbā| dhammī kathāti catusac‍cadhammatau anapaitā kathā, pavattinivattiparidīpinī dhammadaisanāti atthau| dasakathāvatthusaṅkhātāpi hi dhammakathā tadaikadaisā aivāti| ariyauti aikantahitāvahattā ariyau, visuddhau uttamauti vā ariyau| tuṇhībhāvauti samathavipassanābhāvanābhūtaṃ akathanaṃ| kaici pana ‘‘vacīsaṅkhārapaṭipakkhabhāvatau dutiyajjhānaṃ ariyau tuṇhībhāvau’’ti vadanti| aparai ‘‘catutthajjhānaṃ  ariyau tuṇhībhāvau’’ti  vadanti| ayaṃ panaittha atthau – ‘‘bhikkhavai, cittavivaikassa paribrūhanatthaṃ vivaikaṭṭhakāyā suñ‍ñāgārai viharantā sacai kadāci san‍nipatatha, aivaṃ san‍nipatitaihi tumhaihi ‘assutaṃ sāvaiti sutaṃ vā pariyaudapaitī’ti vuttanayaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñassūpakārāya khandhādīnaṃ anic‍catādipaṭisaṃyuttā dhammakathā vā pavattaitabbā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ abyābādhanatthaṃ jhānasamāpattiyā vā viharitabba’’nti|

tattha purimaina karaṇīyavacanaina anautiṇṇānaṃ sāsanai autaraṇūpāyaṃ dassaiti, pacchimaina autiṇṇānaṃ saṃsāratau nissaraṇūpāyaṃ| purimaina vā āgamavaiyyattiyai niyaujaiti, pacchimaina adhigamavaiyyattiyai| atha vā purimaina sammādiṭṭhiyā paṭhamaṃ uppattihaituṃ dīpaiti, dutiyaina dutiyaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, haitū dvai pac‍cayā sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāya paratau ca ghausau, pac‍cattañ‍ca yaunisau manasikārau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 2.127)|

purimaina  vā laukiyasammādiṭṭhiyā mūlakāraṇaṃ vibhāvaiti, pacchimaina laukuttarasammādiṭṭhiyā mūlakāraṇanti aivamādinā aittha yaujanā vaiditabbā|

aitamatthaṃ viditvāti taihi bhikkhūhi kittitakāmasampattitau jhānādisampatti santatarā caiva paṇītatarā cāti aitamatthaṃ sabbākāratau viditvā| imaṃ udānanti imaṃ ariyavihārasukhānubhāvadīpakaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

tattha yañ‍ca kāmasukhaṃ laukaiti laukasaddau ‘‘khandhalaukau āyatanalaukau dhātulaukau’’tiādīsu (mahāni॰ 3, 7; cūḷani॰ ajitamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 2) saṅkhāraisu āgatau|

‘‘yāvatā candimasūriyā pariharanti,

disā bhanti viraucanā|

tāva sahassadhā laukau,

aittha tai vattatī vasau’’ti|| –

ādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.503) aukāsai āgatau| ‘‘addasā khau bhagavā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau’’tiādīsu  (mahāva॰ 9; ma॰ ni॰ 1.283) sattaisu| idha pana  sattalaukai aukāsalaukai ca vaiditabbau| tasmā avīcitau paṭṭhāya upari brahmalaukatau haiṭṭhā aitasmiṃ laukai yaṃ vatthukāmai paṭic‍ca kilaisakāmavasaina uppaj‍janatau kāmasahagataṃ sukhaṃ| yañ‍cidaṃ diviyaṃ sukhanti yañ‍ca idaṃ divi bhavaṃ dibbavihāravasaina ca laddhabbaṃ brahmānaṃ manussānañ‍ca rūpasamāpattisukhaṃ| taṇhakkhayasukhassāti yaṃ āgamma taṇhā khīyati, taṃ nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā taṇhāya ca paṭipassambhanavasaina pavattaphalasamāpattisukhaṃ taṇhakkhayasukhaṃ nāma, tassa taṇhakkhayasukhassa| aitaiti liṅgavipal‍lāsaina niddaisau, aitāni sukhānīti atthau| kaici ubhayampi sukhasāmañ‍ñaina gahaitvā ‘‘aita’’nti paṭhanti, taisaṃ ‘‘kalaṃ nāgghatī’’ti pāṭhaina bhavitabbaṃ|

sauḷasinti sauḷasan‍naṃ pūraṇiṃ| ayañhaittha saṅkhaipatthau – cak‍kavattisukhaṃ ādiṃ katvā sabbasmiṃ manussalaukai manussasukhaṃ, nāgasupaṇṇādilaukai nāgādīhi anubhavitabbaṃ sukhaṃ, cātumahārājikādidaivalaukai chabbidhaṃ kāmasukhanti yaṃ aikādasavidhai kāmalaukai uppaj‍jantaṃ kāmasukhaṃ, yañ‍ca idaṃ rūpārūpadaivaisu dibbavihārabhūtaisu rūpārūpajjhānaisu ca uppan‍nattā ‘‘diviya’’nti  laddhanāmaṃ laukiyajjhānasukhaṃ, sakalampi tadubhayaṃ taṇhakkhayasukhasaṅkhātaṃ phalasamāpattisukhaṃ sauḷasa bhāgai katvā tatau aikabhāgaṃ sauḷasabhāgaguṇai laddhaṃ aikabhāgasaṅkhātaṃ kalaṃ na agghatīti|

ayañ‍ca atthavaṇṇanā phalasamāpattisāmañ‍ñaina vuttā| pāḷiyaṃ avisaisaina taṇhakkhayassa āgatattā paṭhamaphalasamāpattisukhassāpi kalaṃ laukiyaṃ na agghati aiva| tathā hi vuttaṃ –

‘‘pathabyā aikaraj‍jaina, saggassa gamanaina vā|

sabbalaukādhipac‍caina, sautāpattiphalaṃ vara’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 178)|

sautāpattisaṃyuttaipi vuttaṃ –

‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sahabyataṃ, sau tattha nandanai vanai accharāsaṅghaparivutau dibbaihi ca pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti, sau catūhi dhammaihi asaman‍nāgatau| atha khau sau aparimuttauva  nirayā, aparimuttau  tiracchānayauniyā, aparimuttau paittivisayā, aparimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā| kiñ‍cāpi, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau piṇḍiyālaupaina yāpaiti, nantakāni ca dhāraiti, sau catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, atha khau sau parimuttau nirayā, parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā, parimuttau paittivisayā, parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ…pai॰.. buddhau bhagavā’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina…pai॰… viñ‍ñūhī’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina…pai॰… puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti| yau ca, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ paṭilābhau, yau catun‍naṃ  dhammānaṃ paṭilābhau, catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ paṭilābhau catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhassa kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasi’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.997)|

aivaṃ bhagavā sabbattha laukiyasukhaṃ sauttaraṃ sātisayaṃ, laukuttarasukhamaiva anuttaranti atisayanti bhājaisīti|

dutiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. daṇḍasuttavaṇṇanā

13. tatiyai kumārakāti dārakā| antarā ca sāvatthiṃ antarā ca jaitavananti antarāsaddau ‘‘tadantaraṃ kau jānaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgatā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.44; 10.75), ‘‘janā saṅgamma mantainti, mañ‍ca tvañ‍ca kimantara’’ntiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.228) kāraṇai āgatau| ‘‘addasā maṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatarā itthī vij‍jantarikāya bhājanaṃ dhauvantī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.149) khaṇai| ‘‘yassantaratau na santi kaupā’’tiādīsu (udā॰ 20) cittai| ‘‘antarā vausānamāpādī’’tiādīsu  vaimajjhai| ‘‘apicāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, tapaudā dvin‍naṃ mahānirayānaṃ antarikāya āgacchatī’’tiādīsu (pārā॰ 231) vivarai| svāyamidhāpi vivarai vaiditabbau| tasmā sāvatthiyā ca jaitavanassa ca vivaraiti, aivamaittha atthau vaiditabbau| antarāsaddayaugatau caittha upayaugavacanaṃ ‘‘antarā ca sāvatthiṃ antarā ca jaitavana’’nti| īdisaisu ṭhānaisu akkharacintakā ‘‘antarā gāmañ‍ca nadiñ‍ca gacchatī’’ti aikamaiva antarāsaddaṃ payuj‍janti, sau dutiyapadainapi yaujaitabbau hauti| idha pana yaujaitvā vuttau|

ahiṃ daṇḍaina hanantīti bilatau nikkhamitvā gaucarāya gacchantaṃ kaṇhasappaṃ chātajjhattaṃ anubandhitvā yaṭṭhīhi pauthainti| taina ca samayaina bhagavā sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya gacchantau antarāmaggai tai dārakai ahiṃ daṇḍaina hanantai disvā ‘‘kasmā kumārakā imaṃ ahiṃ daṇḍaina hanathā’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘ḍaṃsanabhayaina, bhantai’’ti ca vuttai ‘‘imai attanau sukhaṃ karissāmāti imaṃ paharantā nibbattaṭṭhānai dukkhaṃ anubhavissanti, ahau avij‍jāya nikatikausal‍la’’nti dhammasaṃvaigaṃ uppādaisi| tainaiva ca dhammasaṃvaigaina udānaṃ udānaisi| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘atha khau bhagavā’’tiādi|

tattha  aitamatthaṃ viditvāti ‘‘imai dārakā attasukhāya paradukkhaṃ karauntā sayaṃ parattha sukhaṃ na labhissantī’’ti aitamatthaṃ jānitvāti aivamaikai vaṇṇainti| añ‍ñaisaṃ duppaṭipan‍nānaṃ sukhapariyaisanaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhāya saṃvattati, suppaṭipan‍nānaṃ aikantaina sukhāya saṃvattati| tasmā ‘‘paravihaisāvinimuttā ac‍cantamaiva sukhabhāginau vata mayhaṃ auvādappaṭikarā’’ti saumanassavasainaivaitampi satthā udānaṃ udānaisīti vadanti| aparai pana bhaṇanti ‘‘aivaṃ taihi kumārakaihi pavattitaṃ paravihaiṭhanaṃ sabbākāraina ādīnavatau viditvā paravihaisāya  parānukampāya ca yathāk‍kamaṃ ādīnavānisaṃsavibhāvanaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisī’’ti|

tattha sukhakāmānīti aikantainaiva attanau sukhassa icchanatau sukhānugiddhāni| bhūtānīti pāṇinau| yau daṇḍaina vihiṃsatīti aittha daṇḍaināti daisanāmattaṃ, daṇḍaina vā laiḍḍusatthapāṇippahārādīhi vāti atthau| atha vā daṇḍaināti daṇḍanaina| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yau sukhakāmāni sabbabhūtāni jātiādinā ghaṭṭanavasaina vacīdaṇḍaina vā pāṇimuggarasatthādīhi pauthanatāḷanacchaidanādivasaina sarīradaṇḍaina vā sataṃ vā sahassaṃ vā ṭhāpanavasaina dhanadaṇḍaina vāti imaisu daṇḍaisu yaina kainaci daṇḍaina vihiṃsati vihaiṭhaiti dukkhaṃ pāpaiti, attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau na labhatai sukhanti sau puggalau attanau sukhaṃ aisantau gavaisantau patthaintau  paic‍ca paralaukai manussasukhaṃ dibbasukhaṃ nibbānasukhanti tividhampi sukhaṃ na labhati, añ‍ñadatthu taina daṇḍaina dukkhamaiva labhatīti atthau|

paic‍ca sau labhatai sukhanti yau khantimaittānuddayasampan‍nau ‘‘yathāhaṃ sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭikūlau, aivaṃ sabbaipī’’ti cintaitvā sampattaviratiādīsu ṭhitau vuttanayaina kainaci daṇḍaina sabbānipi bhūtāni na hiṃsati na bādhati, sau puggalau paralaukai manussabhūtau manussasukhaṃ, daivabhūtau dibbasukhaṃ, ubhayaṃ atik‍kamantau nibbānasukhaṃ labhatīti| aittha ca tādisassa puggalassa avassaṃbhāvitāya taṃ sukhaṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ viya hautīti dassanatthaṃ ‘‘labhatai’’ti vuttaṃ| purimagāthāyapi aisaiva nayau|

tatiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. sak‍kārasuttavaṇṇanā

14. catutthai  taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sak‍katau hautīti kappānaṃ satasahassādhikaisu catūsu asaṅkhyaiyyaisu paripūritassa puñ‍ñasambhāravisaisassa phalabhūtaina ‘‘itau paraṃ mayhaṃ aukāsau natthī’’ti ussahajātaina viya uparūpari vaḍḍhamānaina sak‍kārādinā bhagavā sak‍katau hauti| sabbadisāsu hi yamakamahāmaighau vuṭṭhahitvā mahaughaṃ viya sabbapāramiyau ‘‘aikasmiṃ attabhāvai vipākaṃ dassāmā’’ti sampiṇḍitā viya bhagavatau lābhasak‍kāramahaughaṃ nibbattayiṃsu| tatau an‍napānavatthayānamālāgandhavilaipanādihatthā khattiyabrāhmaṇādayau āgantvā ‘‘kahaṃ buddhau, kahaṃ bhagavā, kahaṃ daivadaivau, kahaṃ narāsabhau, kahaṃ purisasīhau’’ti bhagavantaṃ pariyaisanti| sakaṭasataihi pac‍cayai āharitvā aukāsaṃ alabhamānā samantā gāvutappamāṇaipi sakaṭadhuraina sakaṭadhuraṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhanti caiva anubandhanti ca andhakavindabrāhmaṇādayau viya| sabbaṃ taṃ khandhakai (mahāva॰ 282) taisu taisu ca suttaisu āgatanayaina vaiditabbaṃ| yathā ca bhagavatau, aivaṃ bhikkhusaṅghassāti| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘yāvatā khau, cunda, aitarahi saṅghā vā gaṇā vā laukai uppan‍nā, nāhaṃ, cunda, añ‍ñaṃ  aikasaṅghampi samanupassāmi aivaṃ lābhaggayasaggappattaṃ, yatharivāyaṃ, cunda, bhikkhusaṅghau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.176)|

svāyaṃ bhagavatau ca bhikkhusaṅghassa ca uppan‍nau lābhasak‍kārau aikatau hutvā dvin‍naṃ mahānadīnaṃ udakaughau viya appamaiyyau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sak‍katau hauti…pai॰… parikkhārānaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghaupi sak‍katau…pai॰… parikkhārāna’’nti|

titthiyā pana pubbai akatapuñ‍ñatāya ca duppaṭipan‍natāya ca asak‍katā agarukatā, buddhuppādaina pana visaisatau vipan‍nasaubhā sūriyuggamanai khaj‍jaupanakā viya nippabhā  nittaijā hatalābhasak‍kārā ahaisuṃ| tai tādisaṃ bhagavatau saṅghassa ca lābhasak‍kāraṃ asahamānā issāpakatā ‘‘aivaṃ imai pharusāhi vācāhi ghaṭṭaitvāva palāpaissāmā’’ti usūyā visuggāraṃ uggirantā tattha tattha bhikkhū ak‍kausantā paribhāsantā vicariṃsu| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘añ‍ñatitthiyā pana paribbājakā asak‍katā haunti…pai॰… parikkhārānaṃ| atha khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā bhagavatau sak‍kāraṃ asahamānā bhikkhusaṅghassa ca gāmai ca arañ‍ñai ca bhikkhū disvā asabbhāhi pharusāhi vācāhi ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti rausainti vihaisaintī’’ti|

tattha asabbhāhīti asabhāyauggāhi sabhāyaṃ sādhujanasamūhai vattuṃ ayuttāhi, duṭṭhul‍lāhīti atthau| pharusāhīti kakkhaḷāhi mammacchaidikāhi| ak‍kausantīti jātiādīhi ak‍kausavatthūhi khuṃsainti| paribhāsantīti bhaṇḍanavasaina bhayaṃ uppādaintā taj‍jainti| rausaintīti yathā parassa rausau hauti, aivaṃ anuddhaṃsanavasaina rausaṃ uppādainti| vihaisaintīti vihaiṭhainti, vividhaihi ākāraihi aphāsuṃ karaunti|

kathaṃ panaitai samantapāsādikai bhagavati bhikkhusaṅghai ca ak‍kausādīni pavattaisunti? bhagavatau uppādatau pahīnalābhasak‍kāratāya upahatacittā pathaviṃ khaṇitvā pakkhalantā viya avaṇai vaiḷuriyamaṇimhi vaṇaṃ uppādaintā viya ca sundarikaṃ nāma paribbājikaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā tāya satthu bhikkhūnañ‍ca avaṇṇaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā ak‍kausādīni pavattaisuṃ| taṃ panaitaṃ sundarīvatthu paratau sundarīsuttai (udā॰ 38) pāḷiyaṃyaiva āgamissati, tasmā yamaittha vattabbaṃ, taṃ tatthaiva vaṇṇayissāma|

bhikkhū  bhagavatau santikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā taṃ pavattimāraucaisuṃ| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu…pai॰… vihaisaintī’’ti| taṃ vuttatthamaiva|

aitamatthaṃ  viditvāti aitaṃ issāpakatānaṃ titthiyānaṃ vippaṭipattiṃ sabbākāratau viditvā| imaṃ udānanti imaṃ taihi katai vippakārai pasan‍nacittaihi ca paraihi katai upakārai tādibhāvānubhāvadīpakaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

tattha gāmai arañ‍ñai sukhadukkhaphuṭṭhauti gāmai vā arañ‍ñai vā yattha katthaci sukhaina dukkhaina ca phuṭṭhau sukhadukkhāni anubhavantau, taisaṃ vā pac‍cayaihi samaṅgībhūtau| naivattatau nau paratau dahaithāti ‘‘ahaṃ sukhitau, ahaṃ dukkhitau, mama sukhaṃ, mama dukkhaṃ, parainidaṃ mayhaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ uppādita’’nti ca naiva attatau na paratau taṃ sukhadukkhaṃ ṭhapaitha| kasmā? na haittha khandhapañ‍cakai ahanti vā mamanti vā parauti vā parassāti vā passitabbayuttakaṃ kiñ‍ci atthi, kaivalaṃ saṅkhārā aiva pana yathāpac‍cayaṃ uppaj‍jitvā khaṇai khaṇai bhij‍jantīti| sukhadukkhaggahaṇañ‍caittha daisanāsīsaṃ, sabbassāpi laukadhammassa vasaina atthau vaiditabbau| iti bhagavā ‘‘nāhaṃ k‍vacani, kassaci kiñ‍canatasmiṃ, na ca mama k‍vacani, katthaci kiñ‍canatatthī’’ti catukauṭikaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ vibhāvaisi|

idāni tassa attatau paratau ca adahanassa kāraṇaṃ dassaiti ‘‘phusanti phassā upadhiṃ paṭic‍cā’’ti| aitai sukhavaidanīyā dukkhavaidanīyā ca phassā nāma khandhapañ‍cakasaṅkhātaṃ upadhiṃ paṭic‍ca tasmiṃ sati yathāsakaṃ visayaṃ phusanti, tattha pavattantiyaiva| adukkhamasukhā hi vaidanā santasabhāvatāya sukhai aiva saṅgahaṃ gacchatīti duvidhasamphassavasainaivāyaṃ atthavaṇṇanā katā|

yathā pana tai phassā na phusanti, taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘nirupadhiṃ kaina phusaiyyuṃ phassā’’ti vuttaṃ| sabbasau hi khandhūpadhiyā asati kaina kāraṇaina tai phassā phusaiyyuṃ, na taṃ kāraṇaṃ atthi| yadi hi tumhai ak‍kausādivasaina uppaj‍janasukhadukkhaṃ na icchatha, sabbasau nirupadhibhāvaiyaiva  yaugaṃ karaiyyāthāti anupādisaisanibbānadhātuyā gāthaṃ niṭṭhapaisi| aivaṃ iminā udānaina vaṭṭavivaṭṭaṃ kathitaṃ|

catutthasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. upāsakasuttavaṇṇanā

15. pañ‍camai  icchānaṅgalakauti icchānaṅgalanāmakau kausalaisu aikau brāhmaṇagāmau, taṃnivāsitāya tattha vā jātau bhavauti vā icchānaṅgalakau| upāsakauti tīhi saraṇagamanaihi bhagavatau santikai upāsakabhāvassa pavaiditattā upāsakau pañ‍casikkhāpadikau buddhamāmakau, dhammamāmakau, saṅghamāmakau| kainacidaiva karaṇīyaināti uddhārasaudhāpanādinā  kainacidaiva kattabbaina| tīraitvāti niṭṭhāpaitvā| ayaṃ kira upāsakau pubbai abhiṇhaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā payirupāsati, sau katipayaṃ kālaṃ bahukaraṇīyatāya satthu dassanaṃ nābhisambhausi| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘cirassaṃ khau tvaṃ, upāsaka, imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi, yadidaṃ idhāgamanāyā’’ti|

tattha cirassanti ciraina| pariyāyanti vāraṃ| yadidanti nipātau, yau ayanti atthau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – idha mama santikai āgamanāya yau ayaṃ aj‍ja katau vārau, taṃ imaṃ ciraina papañ‍caṃ katvā akāsīti| cirapaṭikāhanti cirapaṭikau ahaṃ, cirakālatau paṭṭhāya ahaṃ upasaṅkamitukāmauti sambandhau| kaihici kaihicīti aikac‍caihi aikac‍caihi| atha vā kaihici kaihicīti yaihi vā taihi vā| tattha gāravaṃ dassaiti| satthari abhippasan‍nassa hi satthudassanadhammassavanaisu viya na añ‍ñattha ādarau hauti| kic‍cakaraṇīyaihīti  aittha avassaṃ kātabbaṃ kic‍caṃ, itaraṃ karaṇīyaṃ| paṭhamaṃ vā kātabbaṃ kic‍caṃ, pacchā kātabbaṃ karaṇīyaṃ| khuddakaṃ vā kic‍caṃ, mahantaṃ karaṇīyaṃ| byāvaṭauti ussuk‍kau| aivāhanti aivaṃ iminā pakāraina ahaṃ nāsakkhiṃ upasaṅkamituṃ, na agāravādināti adhippāyau|

aitamatthaṃ viditvāti dul‍labhai buddhuppādai manussattalābhai ca sattānaṃ sakiñ‍canabhāvaina kic‍capasutatāya kusalantarāyau hauti, na akiñ‍canassāti aitamatthaṃ sabbākāratau viditvā| imaṃ udānanti tadatthaparidīpanamaiva imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

tattha sukhaṃ vata tassa na hauti kiñ‍cīti yassa puggalassa kiñ‍ci rūpādīsu aikavatthumpi ‘‘mamaita’’nti taṇhāya pariggahitabhāvaina na hauti natthi na vij‍jati, sukhaṃ vata tassa puggalassa, ahau sukhamaivāti atthau| ‘‘na hausī’’tipi pāṭhau, tassa atītakālavasaina atthau vaiditabbau| kaici pana na hauti kiñ‍cīti padassa ‘‘rāgādikiñ‍canaṃ yassa na hautī’’ti atthaṃ vaṇṇainti, taṃ na sundaraṃ pariggahadhammavasaina daisanāya āgatattā| rāgādikiñ‍cananti pariggahaitabbassāpi saṅgahai sati yuttamaiva vuttaṃ siyā atha vā yassa puggalassa  kiñ‍ci appampi kiñ‍canaṃ palibaudhajātaṃ rāgādikiñ‍canābhāvatau aiva na hauti, taṃ tassa akiñ‍canattaṃ sukhassa pac‍cayabhāvatau  sukhaṃ vataṃ, ahau sukhanti atthau| kassa pana na hauti kiñ‍cananti cai, āha ‘‘saṅkhātadhammassa bahussutassā’’ti| yau catūhipi maggasaṅkhāhi sauḷasakic‍canipphattiyā saṅkhātadhammau katakic‍cau, tatau aiva paṭivaidhabāhusac‍caina bahussutau, tassa|

iti bhagavā akiñ‍canabhāvai ānisaṃsaṃ dassaitvā sakiñ‍canabhāvai ādīnavaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘sakiñ‍canaṃ passā’’tiādimāha| tassatthau – rāgādikiñ‍canānaṃ āmisakiñ‍canānañ‍ca atthitāya sakiñ‍canaṃ, sakiñ‍canattā aiva aladdhānañ‍ca laddhānañ‍ca kāmānaṃ pariyaisanārakkhaṇahaitu kic‍cakaraṇīyavasaina ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti gahaṇavasaina ca vihañ‍ñamānaṃ vighātaṃ āpaj‍jamānaṃ passāti dhammasaṃvaigappattau satthā attanau  cittaṃ vadati| janau janasmiṃ paṭibandharūpauti sayaṃ añ‍ñau janau samānau añ‍ñasmiṃ janai ‘‘ahaṃ imassa, mama aya’’nti taṇhāvasaina paṭibandhasabhāvau hutvā vihañ‍ñati vighātaṃ āpaj‍jati| ‘‘paṭibaddhacittau’’tipi pāṭhau| ayañ‍ca atthau –

‘‘puttā matthi dhanammatthi, iti bālau vihañ‍ñati|

attā hi attanau natthi, kutau puttā kutau dhana’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 62) –

ādīhi suttapadaihi dīpaitabbauti|

pañ‍camasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. gabbhinīsuttavaṇṇanā

16. chaṭṭhai añ‍ñatarassa paribbājakassāti aikassa kuṭumbikassa paribbājakassa| daharāti taruṇī| māṇavikāti brāhmaṇadhītāya vauhārau| pajāpatīti bhariyā| gabbhinīti āpan‍nasattā| upavijañ‍ñāti aj‍ja suvaiti pac‍cupaṭṭhitavijāyanakālā hautīti sambandhau| sau kira brāhmaṇajātikau sabhariyau vādapatthassamai ṭhitau, taina naṃ sapajāpatikaṃ paribbājakavauhāraina  samudācaranti| bhariyā panassa brāhmaṇajātikattā brāhmaṇāti ālapati| tailanti tilatailaṃ| tailasīsaina caittha yaṃ yaṃ vijātāya pasavadukkhappaṭikāratthaṃ icchitabbaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ sappilauṇādiṃ āharāti āṇāpaiti| yaṃ mai vijātāya bhavissatīti  yaṃ tailādi mayhaṃ vijātāya bahinikkhantagabbhāya upakārāya bhavissati| ‘‘paribbājikāyā’’tipi pāṭhau| kutauti kasmā ṭhānā, yatau ñātikulā vā mittakulā vā tailādiṃ āharaiyyaṃ, taṃ ṭhānaṃ mai natthīti adhippāyau| tailaṃ  āharāmīti vattamānasamīpatāya vattamānaṃ katvā vuttaṃ, tailaṃ āharissāmīti atthau| samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā sappissa vā tailassa vāti ca samuc‍cayatthau vā-saddau ‘‘aggitau vā udakatau vā mithubhaidā vā’’tiādīsu (mahāva॰ 286; dī॰ ni॰ 2.152; udā॰ 76) viya| sappissa vā tailassa vāti pac‍cattai sāmivacanaṃ, sappi ca tailañ‍ca yāvadatthaṃ pātuṃ pivituṃ dīyatīti atthau| aparai pana ‘‘sappissa vā tailassa vāti avayavasambandhai sāmivacanaṃ| sappitailasamudāyassa hi avayavau idha yāvadatthasaddaina vuc‍catī’’ti vadanti| nau nīharitunti bhājanaina vā hatthaina vā bahi naituṃ nau dīyati, ucchadditvānāti vamitvā, yaṃnūna dadaiyyanti sambandhau| aivaṃ kirassa ahausi ‘‘ahaṃ rañ‍ñau kauṭṭhāgāraṃ gantvā tailaṃ kaṇṭhamattaṃ pivitvā tāvadaiva gharaṃ āgantvā aikasmiṃ bhājanai yathāpītaṃ vamitvā uddhanaṃ āraupaitvā pacissāmi, yaṃ pittasaimhādimissitaṃ, taṃ agginā jhāyissati, tailaṃ pana gahaitvā imissā paribbājikāya kammai upanaissāmī’’ti|

uddhaṃ kātunti vamanavasaina uddhaṃ nīharituṃ| na pana adhauti viriñ‍canavasaina haiṭṭhā nīharituṃ na pana sak‍kauti| sau hi ‘‘adhikaṃ pītaṃ sayamaiva mukhatau niggamissatī’’ti pivitvā āsayassa arittatāya aniggatai vamanaviraicanayaugaṃ ajānantau alabhantau vā kaivalaṃ dukkhāhi vaidanāhi phuṭṭhau āvaṭṭati ca parivaṭṭati ca| dukkhāhīti dukkhamāhi| tibbāhīti bahalāhi tikhiṇāhi vā| kharāhīti kakkhaḷāhi| kaṭukāhīti ativiya aniṭṭhabhāvaina dāruṇāhi| āvaṭṭatīti aikasmiṃyaiva ṭhānai anipaj‍jitvā attanau sarīraṃ itau citau ākaḍḍhantau āvaṭṭati| parivaṭṭatīti aikasmiṃ padaisai nipan‍naupi aṅgapac‍caṅgāni paritau khipantau vaṭṭati, abhimukhaṃ vā vaṭṭantau āvaṭṭati, samantatau vaṭṭantau parivaṭṭati|

aitamatthaṃ viditvāti ‘‘sakiñ‍canassa appaṭisaṅkhāparibhaugahaitukā ayaṃ dukkhuppatti, akiñ‍canassa  pana sabbasau ayaṃ natthī’’ti aitamatthaṃ sabbākāratau jānitvā tadatthappakāsanaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

tattha  sukhinau vatāti sukhinau vata sappurisā| kai pana taiti? yai akiñ‍canā, yai rāgādikiñ‍canassa pariggahakiñ‍canassa ca abhāvaina akiñ‍canā, kaisaṃ panidaṃ kiñ‍canaṃ natthīti āha – ‘‘vaidagunau hi janā akiñ‍canā’’ti, yai ariyamaggañāṇasaṅkhātaṃ vaidaṃ gatā adhigatā, taina vā vaidaina nibbānaṃ gatāti vaidagunau, tai ariyajanā khīṇāsavapuggalā anavasaisarāgādikiñ‍canānaṃ aggamaggaina samucchin‍nattā akiñ‍canā nāma| asati hi rāgādikiñ‍canai kutau pariggahakiñ‍canassa sambhavau| aivaṃ gāthāya purimabhāgaina arahantai pasaṃsitvā aparabhāgaina andhaputhuj‍janai garahantau ‘‘sakiñ‍canaṃ passā’’tiādimāha| taṃ purimasuttai vuttatthamaiva| aivaṃ imāyapi gāthāya vaṭṭavivaṭṭaṃ kathitaṃ|

chaṭṭhasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. aikaputtakasuttavaṇṇanā

17. sattamai aikaputtakauti aikau puttau, sau ca anukampitabbaṭṭhaina aikaputtakau, piyāyitabbaṭṭhaina piyau, manassa vaḍḍhanaṭṭhaina manāpau| sarīrasaubhāsampattiyā vā dassanīyaṭṭhaina piyau, sīlācārasampattiyā kalyāṇadhammatāya manāpau| kalaiti sattai khaipaitīti kālau, maraṇaṃ| taṃ katau pattauti kālaṅkatau, kālaina vā mac‍cunā katau naṭṭhau adassanaṃ gatauti kālaṅkatau, matauti atthau|

sambahulā upāsakāti sāvatthivāsinau bahū upāsakā mataputtaupāsakassa sahasaukībhāvaina yāva āḷāhanā pacchatau gantvā matasarīrassa kattabbaṃ kāraitvā paṭinivattā yathānivatthāva udakaṃ autaritvā sīsaṃnhātā vatthāni pīḷaitvā anautāpaitvāva aikaṃ nivāsaitvā  aikaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ katvā upāsakaṃ puratau katvā ‘‘saukavinaudanaṃ dhammaṃ satthu santikai saussāmā’’ti bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamiṃsu| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘al‍lakaisā’’tiādi|

tattha al‍lavatthāti udakaina tintavatthā| divā divassāti divasassapi divā, majjhanhikai kālaiti atthau| yasmā jānantāpi tathāgatā pucchanti, jānantāpi na pucchanti| kālaṃ viditvā pucchanti, kālaṃ viditvā na pucchanti, tasmā jānantauyaiva bhagavā kathāsamuṭṭhāpanatthaṃ pucchantau  ‘‘kiṃ nu khau tumhai upāsakā’’tiādimāha| tassatthau – tumhai upāsakā añ‍ñaisu divasaisu mama santikaṃ āgacchantā autāpitasuddhavatthā sāyanhai āgacchatha, aj‍ja pana al‍lavatthā al‍lakaisā ṭhitamajjhanhikai kālai idhāgatā, taṃ kiṃ kāraṇanti| taina mayanti taina puttaviyaugajanitacittasantāpaina balavasaukābhibhūtatāya aivaṃbhūtā mayaṃ idhūpasaṅkamantāti|

aitamatthaṃ viditvāti piyavatthusambhavā saukadukkhadaumanassādayau, asati piyavatthusmiṃ sabbasau aitai na santīti aitamatthaṃ sabbākāratau jānitvā tadatthappakāsanaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

tattha piyarūpassādagadhitāsaiti piyasabhāvaisu rūpakkhandhādīsu sukhavaidanassādaina gadhitā paṭibaddhacittā| gadhitāsaiti hi gadhitāic‍caivatthau| saiti vā nipātamattaṃ| piyarūpā nāma cakkhādayau puttadārādayau ca| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘kiñ‍ca laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ cakkhu laukai …pai॰… dhammataṇhā laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpa’’nti (cūḷani॰ haimakamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 55)|

‘‘khaittaṃ vatthuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ vā, gavassaṃ dāsapaurisaṃ|

thiyau bandhū puthu kāmai, yau narau anugijjhatī’’ti ca|| (su॰ ni॰ 775)|

tasmā taisu piyarūpaisu assādaina giddhā mucchitā ajjhāpan‍nāti atthau| kai pana tai piyarūpassādagadhitāti tai dassaiti ‘‘daivakāyā  puthumanussā cā’’ti, cātumahārājikādayau bahudaivasamūhā caiva jambudīpakādikā bahumanussā ca| aghāvinauti kāyikacaitasikadukkhaina dukkhitā| parijun‍nāti jarāraugādivipattiyā yaubbanāraugyādisampattitau parihīnā| yathālābhavasaina vāyamatthau daivamanussaisu vaiditabbau| atha vā kāmañ‍caikantasukhasamappitānaṃ daivānaṃ dukkhajarāraugā na sambhavanti, tadanativattasabhāvatāya pana taipi ‘‘aghāvinau’’ti ‘‘parijun‍nā’’ti ca vuttā| taisampi vā pubbanimittuppattiyā paṭicchan‍najarāya caitasikaraugassa ca vasaina dukkhādīnaṃ sambhavau vaiditabbau| mac‍curājassa vasaṃ gacchantīti piyavatthuvisayāya taṇhāya appahīnattā punappunaṃ gabbhūpagamanatau  dhātuttayissaratāya mac‍curājasaṅkhātassa maraṇassa vasaṃ hatthamaiva gacchanti|

aittāvatā vaṭṭaṃ dassaitvā idāni ‘‘yai vai divā’’tiādinā vivaṭṭaṃ dassaiti| tattha yai vai divā ca rattau ca appamattāti ‘‘divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’tiādinā  vuttanayaina divasabhāgai rattibhāgai ca daḷhaṃ appamattā appamādappaṭipadaṃ pūrainti| jahanti piyarūpanti catusac‍cakammaṭṭhānabhāvanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā ariyamaggādhigamaina piyarūpaṃ piyajātikaṃ cakkhādipiyavatthuṃ tappaṭibaddhachandarāgajahanaina jahanti| tai vai khaṇanti aghamūlaṃ, mac‍cunau āmisaṃ durativattanti tai ariyapuggalā aghassa vaṭṭadukkhassa mūlabhūtaṃ, mac‍cunā maraṇaina āmasitabbatau āmisaṃ, itau bahiddhā kaihicipi samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi nivattituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyatāya durativattaṃ, saha avij‍jāya taṇhaṃ ariyamaggañāṇakudālaina khaṇanti, laisamattampi anavasaisantā ummūlayantīti| svāyamatthau –

‘‘appamādau  amatapadaṃ, pamādau mac‍cunau padaṃ|

appamattā na mīyanti, yai pamattā yathā matā’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 21) –

ādīhi suttapadaihi vitthāraitabbauti|

sattamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. suppavāsāsuttavaṇṇanā

18. aṭṭhamai kuṇḍikāyanti aivaṃnāmakai kauliyānaṃ nagarai| kuṇḍadhānavanaiti tassa nagarassa avidūrai kuṇḍadhānasaṅkhātai vanai|

pubbai kira kuṇḍau nāma aikau yakkhau tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai vāsaṃ kappaisi, kuṇḍadhānamissakaina ca balikammaina tussatīti tassa tathā tattha baliṃ upaharanti, tainaitaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ kuṇḍadhānavanantvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha| tassa avidūrai aikā gāmapatikā ahausi, sāpi tassa yakkhassa āṇāpavattiṭṭhānai niviṭṭhattā tainaiva paripālitattā kuṇḍikāti vauharīyittha| aparabhāgai tattha kauliyarājānau nagaraṃ kāraisuṃ, tampi purimavauhāraina kuṇḍikātvaiva vuc‍cati| tasmiñ‍ca vanasaṇḍai kauliyarājānau bhagavatau bhikkhusaṅghassa ca vasanatthāya vihāraṃ patiṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tampi kuṇḍadhānavanantvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha| atha bhagavā janapadacārikaṃ carantau anuk‍kamaina taṃ vihāraṃ  patvā tattha vihāsi| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kuṇḍikāyaṃ viharati kuṇḍadhānavanai’’ti|

suppavāsāti tassā upāsikāya nāmaṃ| kauliyadhītāti kauliyarājaputtī| sā hi bhagavatau aggupaṭṭhāyikā paṇītadāyikānaṃ sāvikānaṃ aitadaggai ṭhapitā sautāpan‍nā ariyasāvikā| yañhi kiñ‍ci bhagavatau yuttarūpaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ vā na tattha añ‍ñāhi saṃvidhātabbaṃ atthi, sabbaṃ taṃ sayamaiva attanau pañ‍ñāya vicāraitvā sak‍kac‍caṃ sampādaitvā  upanaiti| daivasikañ‍ca aṭṭhasataṃ saṅghabhattapāṭipuggalikabhattāni daiti| yau kauci bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā taṃ kulaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau rittahatthau na gacchati| aivaṃ muttacāgā payatapāṇī vaussaggaratā yācayaugā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā| assā kucchiyaṃ purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau pacchimabhavikau sāvakabaudhisattau paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| sā taṃ gabbhaṃ kainacidaiva pāpakammaina satta vassāni kucchinā parihari, sattāhañ‍ca mūḷhagabbhā ahausi| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘satta vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraiti sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā’’ti|

tattha satta vassānīti satta saṃvaccharāni, ac‍cantasaṃyaugai ca idaṃ upayaugavacanaṃ| gabbhaṃ dhāraitīti gabbhaṃ vahati, gabbhinī hautīti atthau| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhāti satta ahāni byākulagabbhā| gabbhau hi paripak‍kau sampaj‍jamānau vijāyanakālai kammajavātaihi sañ‍cālaitvā parivattitau uddhaṃpādau adhausirau hutvā yaunimukhābhimukhau hauti, aivaṃ sau katthaci alaggau bahi nikkhamati| vipaj‍jamānau pana viparivattanavasaina yaunimaggaṃ pidahitvā tiriyaṃ nipaj‍jati, sayamaiva vā yaunimaggau pidahati, sau tattha kammajavātaihi aparāparaṃ parivattamānau byākulau mūḷhagabbhauti vuc‍cati| tassāpi satta divasai aivaṃ ahausi, taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā’’ti|

ayañ‍ca gabbhau sīvalitthairau| tassa kathaṃ satta vassāni gabbhavāsadukkhaṃ, sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhabhāvappatti, mātu cassāpi sautāpan‍nāya ariyasāvikāya  tathā dukkhānubhavanaṃ jātanti? vuc‍catai – atītai kāsikarājai bārāṇasiyaṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraintai aikau kausalarājā mahantaina balaināgantvā bārāṇasiṃ gahaitvā taṃ rājānaṃ māraitvā tassa aggamahaisiṃ attanau aggamahaisiṃ akāsi| bārāṇasirañ‍ñau pana puttau pitu maraṇakālai niddhamanadvāraina palāyitvā attanau ñātimittabandhavai aikajjhaṃ katvā anuk‍kamaina balaṃ saṃharitvā bārāṇasiṃ āgantvā avidūrai mahantaṃ khandhāvāraṃ bandhitvā tassa rañ‍ñau paṇṇaṃ paisaisi ‘‘raj‍jaṃ vā daitu, yuddhaṃ vā’’ti| rājakumārassa  mātā sāsanaṃ sutvā ‘‘yuddhaina kammaṃ natthi, sabbadisāsu sañ‍cāraṃ pacchinditvā bārāṇasinagaraṃ parivāraitu, tatau dārūdakabhattaparikkhayaina kilantā nagarai manussā vināva yuddhaina rājānaṃ gahaitvā dassantī’’ti paṇṇaṃ paisaisi| sau mātu sāsanaṃ sutvā cattāri mahādvārāni rakkhantau satta vassāni nagaraṃ uparundhi, nagarai manussā cūḷadvāraina nikkhamitvā dārūdakāni āharanti, sabbakic‍cāni karaunti|

atha rājakumārassa mātā taṃ pavattiṃ sutvā ‘‘bālau mama puttau upāyaṃ na jānāti, gacchatha, assa cūḷadvārāni pidhāya nagaraṃ uparundhatūti vadaithā’’ti puttassa gūḷhasāsanaṃ pahiṇi| sau mātu sāsanaṃ sutvā satta divasai tathā akāsi| nāgarā bahi nikkhamituṃ alabhantā sattamai divasai tassa rañ‍ñau sīsaṃ gahaitvā kumārassa adaṃsu| kumārau nagaraṃ pavisitvā raj‍jaṃ aggahaisi| sau tadā satta vassāni nagararundhanakammanissandaina aitarahi satta vassāni mātukucchisaṅkhātāya lauhitakumbhiyā vasi, avasaisatau pana sattāhaṃ nagarūparundhanaina sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhabhāvaṃ āpaj‍ji| jātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘satta divasāni nagaraṃ rundhitvā gahitakammanissandaina sattavassāni lauhitakumbhiyaṃ vasitvā sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jī’’ti vuttaṃ| yaṃ pana sau padumuttarasammāsambuddhapādamūlai ‘‘lābhīnaṃ aggau bhavaiyya’’nti mahādānaṃ datvā patthanaṃ akāsi, yañ‍ca vipassissa bhagavatau kālai nāgaraihi saddhiṃ sahassagghanikaṃ guḷadadhiṃ datvā patthanaṃ akāsi, tassānubhāvaina lābhīnaṃ aggau jātau| suppavāsāpi ‘‘nagaraṃ rundhitvā gaṇha, tātā’’ti paisitabhāvaina satta vassāni kucchinā gabbhaṃ pariharitvā sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhājātā| aivaṃ tai mātāputtā attanau kammassa anurūpaṃ īdisaṃ dukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiṃsu|

tīhi  vitak‍kaihīti ratanattayaguṇānussatipaṭisaṃyuttaihi tīhi sammāvitak‍kaihi| adhivāsaitīti mūḷhagabbhatāya uppan‍nadukkhaṃ sahati| sā hi bhagavatau sambuddhabhāvaṃ, ariyasaṅghassa suppaṭipattiṃ, nibbānassa ca dukkhanissaraṇabhāvaṃ anussarantī attanau uppaj‍jamānadukkhaṃ amanasikaraṇainaiva abhibhavitvā khamati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘tīhi vitak‍kaihi adhivāsaitī’’ti|

sammāsambuddhau vatātiādi taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ pavattiākāradassanaṃ| tassatthau – yau bhagyavantatādīhi kāraṇaihi bhagavā laukanāthau sammā aviparītaṃ sāmaṃ sayamaiva sabbadhammai ahau vata buddhau, sau bhagavā aivarūpassa aitarahi mayā anubhaviyamānassa añ‍ñassa ca aivaṃjātikassa  sakalassa vaṭṭadukkhassa pahānāya ac‍cantaṃ anuppādaniraudhāya dhammaṃ kathaiti, aviparītadhammaṃ kathaiti| aviparītadhammadaisanatāya hi satthu sammāsambaudhisiddhi| tassa yathāvuttaguṇassa bhagavatau dhammassavanantai jātattā sīladiṭṭhisāmañ‍ñaina saṃhatattā ca sāvakasaṅghauti laddhanāmau aṭṭhaariyapuggalasamūhau suppaṭipan‍nau vata ahau vata sammā paṭipan‍nau, yau ariyasaṅghau aivarūpassa īdisassa vaṭṭadukkhassa pahānāya anuppādaniraudhāya anivattipaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau| susukhaṃ vata ahau vata suṭṭhu sukhaṃ sabbasaṅkhatanissaṭaṃ nibbānaṃ, yasmiṃ nibbānai īdisaṃ vaṭṭadukkhaṃ na upalabbhatīti| aittha ca paṭipaj‍jamānāpi paṭipan‍nā ic‍caiva vuttā paṭipattiyā anivattibhāvatau| atha vā uppan‍nasaddau viya paṭipan‍nasaddau vattamānatthaupi vaiditabbau| tainaivāha ‘‘sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau’’ti|

sāmikanti attanau patiṃ kauliyarājaputtaṃ| āmantaisīti abhāsi| mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhīti mayhaṃ vacanaina cak‍kalakkhaṇappaṭimaṇḍitāni vikasitapadumasassirīkāni bhagavatau caraṇāni tava sirasā vandāhi, uttamaṅgaina abhivādanaṃ karauhīti atthau| appābādhantiādīsu ābādhauti visabhāgavaidanā vuc‍cati, yā aikadaisai uppaj‍jitvāpi sakalasarīraṃ ayapaṭṭaina ābandhitvā  viya gaṇhati| ātaṅkauti kicchajīvitakarau raugau| atha vā yāpaitabbaraugau ātaṅkau, itarau ābādhau| khuddakau vā raugau ātaṅkau, balavā  ābādhau| kaici pana ‘‘ajjhattasamuṭṭhānau ābādhau, bahiddhāsamuṭṭhānau ātaṅkau’’ti vadanti| tadubhayassāpi abhāvaṃ pucchāti vadati| gilānassaiva ca uṭṭhānaṃ nāma garukaṃ hauti, kāyai balaṃ na hauti, tasmā niggailañ‍ñatāya lahuparivattisaṅkhātaṃ kāyassa lahuṭṭhānaṃ sarīrabalañ‍ca pucchāti vadati| phāsuvihāranti ṭhānanisin‍nagamanasayanasaṅkhātaisu catūsu iriyāpathaisu sukhavihārañ‍ca pucchāti vadati| athassa pucchitabbākāraṃ dassaintī ‘‘suppavāsā, bhantai’’tiādimāha| aivañ‍ca vadaihīti idāni vattabbākāraṃ nidassaiti|

paramanti vacanasampaṭicchanaṃ| taina sādhu, bhaddai, yathā vuttaṃ, tathā paṭipaj‍jāmīti dassaiti| kauliyaputtauti suppavāsāya sāmikau kauliyarājaputtau| sukhinī hautūti sadaivakai laukai aggadakkhiṇaiyyau satthā suppavāsāya paisitavandanaṃ sampaṭicchitvā tadanantaraṃ attanau maittāvihārasaṃsūcakaṃ buddhāciṇṇaṃ sukhūpasaṃhāraṃ tassā sāmañ‍ñatau pakāsaitvā puna tassā puttassa ca gabbhavipattimūlakadukkhuppattipaṭikkhaipamukhaina sukhūpasaṃhāraṃ nidassaintau ‘‘sukhinī…pai॰… araugā, araugaṃ puttaṃ vijāyatū’’ti āha|

saha  vacanāti bhagavatau vacanaina sahaiva| yasmiṃ kālai bhagavā tathā avauca, tasmiṃyaiva kālai tampi kammaṃ parikkhayaṃ agamāsi| tassa parikkhīṇabhāvaṃ aulaukaitvā satthā tathā abhāsi| aparai pana vadanti – sacai tathā satthā nācikkhissā, tatau parampi kiñ‍ci kālaṃ tassā taṃ dukkhaṃ anubandhissā| yasmā pana bhagavatā ‘‘sukhinī araugā araugañ‍ca puttaṃ vijāyatū’’ti vuttaṃ, tasmā tassa vacanasamakālamaiva sau gabbhau byākulabhāvaṃ vijahitvā sukhainaiva bahi nikkhami, aivaṃ taisaṃ mātāputtānaṃ sautthi ahausi| acintaiyyau hi buddhānaṃ  buddhānubhāvau| yathā hi paṭācārāya piyavippayaugasambhūtaina saukaina ummādaṃ patvā –

‘‘ubhau puttā kālakatā, panthai mayhaṃ patī matau|

mātā pitā ca bhātā ca, aikacitakamhi jhāyarai’’ti|| (apa॰ thairī 2.2.498) –

vatvā  jātarūpainaiva carantiyā ‘‘satiṃ paṭilabhāhi bhaginī’’ti bhagavatau vacanasamanantaramaiva ummādau vūpasami, tathā suppiyāpi upāsikā attanāva attanau ūruyaṃ kataina mahāvaṇaina vuṭṭhātuṃ asak‍kauntī sayanapiṭṭhai nipan‍nā ‘‘āgantvā maṃ vandatū’’ti vacanasamanantaramaiva vaṇai pākatikai jātai sayamaiva gantvā bhagavantaṃ vandīti aivamādīni vatthūni idha udāharitabbānīti|

aivaṃ, bhantaiti, bhantai, yathā bhagavā saputtāya mātuyā araugabhāvaṃ āsīsantau āha – ‘‘sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijāyatū’’ti, taṃ aivamaiva| na hi kadāci buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ vacanassa añ‍ñathābhāvauti adhippāyau| kaici pana ‘‘aivamatthū’’ti vadanti, aparai ‘‘hautū’’ti padassa atthaṃ ānaitvā vaṇṇayanti| abhinanditvāti karavīkarutamañ‍junā brahmassaraina bhagavatā vuc‍camānai tasmiṃ vacanai pītisaumanassapaṭilābhatau abhimukhabhāvaina nanditvā| anumauditvāti tatau pacchāpi sammaudanaṃ uppādaitvā, cittaina vā abhinanditvā vācāya anumauditvā, vacanasampattiyā vā abhinanditvā atthasampattiyā anumauditvā| sakaṃ gharaṃ pac‍cāyāsīti attanau gharaṃ paṭigacchi| yai pana ‘‘yaina sakaṃ ghara’’nti paṭhanti, taisaṃ yadipi ya-ta-saddānaṃ sambandhabhāvatau ‘‘tainā’’ti padaṃ vuttamaiva hauti, tathāpi ‘‘paṭiyāyitvā’’ti pāṭhasaisau yaujaitabbau hauti|

vijātanti pajātaṃ, pasutanti atthau| acchariyanti andhassa pabbatārauhanaṃ viya nic‍caṃ na  hautīti acchariyaṃ, ayaṃ tāva saddanayau| aṭṭhakathāsu pana ‘‘accharāyauggaṃ acchariya’’nti vuttaṃ, accharaṃ paharituṃ yuttanti atthau| vatāti sambhāvanai, ahau acchariyanti atthau| bhauti dhammālapanaṃ| abhūtapubbaṃ bhūtanti abbhutaṃ|

tathāgatassāti aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā tathāgatau – tathā āgatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatauti tathāgatau, tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau, tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau, tathadassitāya tathāgatau, tathavāditāya tathāgatau, tathākāritāya tathāgatau, abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau|

kathaṃ  bhagavā tathā āgatauti tathāgatau? yathā sabbalaukahitāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍nā purimakā sammāsambuddhā āgatāti| kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? yaina abhinīhāraina tai bhagavantau āgatā, taina aṭṭhaguṇasaman‍nāgataina ayampi bhagavā āgatau| yathā ca tai bhagavantau dānapāramiṃ pūraitvā sīlanaikkhammapañ‍ñāvīriyakhantisac‍caadhiṭṭhānamaittāupaikkhāpāramīti imā dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samatiṃsa pāramiyau pūraitvā pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai paric‍cajitvā pubbayaugapubbacariyadhammakkhānañātatthacariyādayau pūraitvā buddhicariyāya kauṭiṃ patvā āgatā, tathā ayampi bhagavā āgatau| yathā ca tai bhagavantau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai…pai॰… ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaitvā brūhaitvā āgatā, tathā ayampi bhagavā āgatau| aivaṃ tathā āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau? yathā sampatijātā tai bhagavantau samaihi pādaihi pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya uttarābhimukhā sattapadavītihāraina gatā, saitacchattai dhāriyamānai sabbāva disā anuvilaukaisuṃ, āsabhiñ‍ca  vācaṃ bhāsiṃsu laukai attanau jaiṭṭhasaiṭṭhabhāvaṃ pakāsaintā, tañ‍ca naisaṃ gamanaṃ tathaṃ ahausi avitathaṃ anaikaisaṃ visaisādhigamānaṃ pubbanimittabhāvaina, tathā ayampi bhagavā gatau, tañ‍cassa gamanaṃ kathaṃ ahausi avitathaṃ taisañ‍ñaiva visaisādhigamānaṃ pubbanimittabhāvaina| aivaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau|

yathā vā tai bhagavantau naikkhammaina kāmacchandaṃ pahāya gatā, abyāpādaina byāpādaṃ, ālaukasañ‍ñāya thinamiddhaṃ, avikkhaipaina uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, dhammavavatthānaina vicikicchaṃ pahāya gatā, ñāṇaina avij‍jaṃ padālaitvā, pāmauj‍jaina aratiṃ vinaudaitvā, aṭṭhasamāpattīhi aṭṭhārasahi mahāvipassanāhi  catūhi ca ariyamaggaihi taṃ taṃ paṭipakkhaṃ pahāya gatā, aivaṃ ayampi bhagavā gatau| aivampi tathā gatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau? pathavīdhātuyā kakkhaḷalakkhaṇaṃ, āpaudhātuyā paggharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ, taijaudhātuyā uṇhattalakkhaṇaṃ, vāyaudhātuyā vitthambhanalakkhaṇaṃ, ākāsadhātuyā asamphuṭṭhalakkhaṇaṃ, rūpassa ruppanalakkhaṇaṃ, vaidanāya vaidayitalakkhaṇaṃ, sañ‍ñāya sañ‍jānanalakkhaṇaṃ, saṅkhārānaṃ abhisaṅkharaṇalakkhaṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇassa vijānanalakkhaṇanti  aivaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ, dvādasan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ, aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ, bāvīsatiyā indriyānaṃ, catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ, dvādasapadikassa pac‍cayākārassa, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ, ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa, sattan‍naṃ visuddhīnaṃ, amataugadhassa nibbānassāti aivaṃ tassa tassa dhammassa yaṃ sabhāvasarasalakkhaṇaṃ, taṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ anañ‍ñathaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ ñāṇagatiyā āgatau avirajjhitvā pattau adhigatauti tathāgatau| aivaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau? tathadhammā nāma cattāri ariyasac‍cāni| yathāha  – ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| katamāni cattāri? idaṃ dukkhanti, bhikkhavai, tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaita’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1090) vitthārau| tāni ca bhagavā abhisambuddhau, tasmā tathānaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatau|

apica jarāmaraṇassa jātipac‍cayasambhūtasamudāgataṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau…pai॰… saṅkhārānaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayasambhūtasamudāgataṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau, tathā avij‍jāya saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayaṭṭhau…pai॰.. jātiyā jarāmaraṇassa pac‍cayaṭṭhau tathau avitathau anañ‍ñathau, taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavā abhisambuddhau, tasmāpi tathānaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatau| abhisambuddhatthau hi aittha gatasaddauti| aivaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau? yaṃ sadaivakai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya pajāya aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ cakkhudvārai āpāthamāgacchantaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ nāma atthi, taṃ bhagavā sabbākāratau jānāti passati| aivaṃ jānatā passatā ca taina taṃ iṭṭhāniṭṭhādivasaina vā diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātaisu labbhamānakapadavasaina vā ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītaka’’ntiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 616) nayaina anaikaihi nāmaihi tairasahi vāraihi dvipañ‍ñāsāya nayaihi vibhaj‍jamānaṃ tathamaiva hauti, vitathaṃ natthi| aisa nayau sautadvārādīsu āpāthamāgacchantaisu saddādīsu| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ jānāmi, tamahaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, taṃ tathāgatassa viditaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau na upaṭṭhāsī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.24)|

aivaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau| tattha tathadassīatthai tathāgatauti padasambhavau vaiditabbau|

kathaṃ tathavāditāya tathāgatau? yaṃ rattiṃ bhagavā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyi, aitthantarai pañ‍cacattālīsavassaparimāṇakālaṃ yaṃ bhagavatā bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ suttagaiyyādi, sabbaṃ taṃ parisuddhaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ rāgamadādinimmathanaṃ aikasadisaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ| taināha –

‘‘yañ‍ca, cunda, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai bhāsati lapati niddisati, sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.188)|

gadaatthau aittha gatasaddau| aivaṃ tathavāditāya tathāgatau|

apica āgadanaṃ āgadau, vacananti atthau| tathau avitathau aviparītau āgadau assāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti aivamaittha padasiddhi vaiditabbā|

kathaṃ tathākāritāya tathāgatau? bhagavatau hi vācāya kāyau anulaumaiti, kāyassapi vācā, tasmā yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī ca hauti| aivaṃbhūtassa cassa yathāvācā, kāyaupi tathā gatau pavattauti atthau| yathā ca kāyau, vācāpi tathā gatā pavattāti tathāgatau| taināha –

‘‘yathāvādī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| iti yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

aivaṃ  tathākāritāya tathāgatau|

kathaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau? yasmā upari bhavaggaṃ haiṭṭhā avīciṃ pariyantaṃ karitvā tiriyaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu sabbasattai abhibhavati sīlainapi samādhināpi pañ‍ñāyapi vimuttiyāpi vimuttiñāṇadassanainapi, na tassa tulā vā pamāṇaṃ vā atthi, atha khau atulau appamaiyyau anuttarau daivadaivau sak‍kānaṃ atisak‍kau, brahmānaṃ atibrahmā sabbasattuttamau, tasmā tathāgatau| taināha –

‘‘sadaivakai, bhikkhavai, laukai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya tathāgatau abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavatti, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

tatrāyaṃ padasiddhi – agadau viya agadau, daisanāvilāsau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca| taina sau mahānubhāvau bhisak‍kau viya dibbāgadaina sappai sabbaparappavādinau sadaivakañ‍ca laukaṃ abhibhavati| iti sabbalaukābhibhavanai tathau avitathau aviparītau yathāvuttauva agadau aitassāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti vaiditabbau| aivaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau|

apica tathāya gatauti tathāgatau, tathaṃ gatauti tathāgatau| tattha sakalalaukaṃ tīraṇapariñ‍ñāya tathāya gatau avagatauti tathāgatau| laukasamudayaṃ pahānapariñ‍ñāya tathāya gatau atītauti tathāgatau| laukaniraudhaṃ sacchikiriyāya tathāya gatau adhigatauti tathāgatau| laukaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ tathaṃ gatau paṭipan‍nauti tathāgatau| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘laukau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukasmā tathāgatau visaṃyuttau| laukasamudayau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukasamudayau tathāgatassa pahīnau, laukaniraudhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukaniraudhau tathāgatassa sacchikatau| laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā tathāgatassa bhāvitā| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa…pai॰… sabbaṃ taṃ tathāgataina abhisambuddhaṃ| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

aparaihipi aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā tathāgatau – tathāya āgatauti tathāgatau, tathāya gatauti tathāgatau, tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatauti tathāgatau, tathāvidhauti tathāgatau, tathā pavattitauti tathāgatau, tathaihi agatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatabhāvaina tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathāya āgatauti tathāgatau? yā sā bhagavatā sumaidhabhūtaina dīpaṅkaradasabalassa pādamūlai –

‘‘manussattaṃ liṅgasampatti, haitu satthāradassanaṃ,

pabbaj‍jā guṇasampatti, adhikārau ca chandatā|

aṭṭhadhammasamaudhānā, abhinīhārau samijjhatī’’ti|| (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.59) –

aivaṃ vuttaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ abhinīhāraṃ sampādaintaina ‘‘ahaṃ sadaivakaṃ laukaṃ tiṇṇau tāraissāmi, muttau maucaissāmi, dantau damaissāmi, santau samaissāmi, assatthau assāsaissāmi, parinibbutau parinibbāpaissāmi, buddhau baudhaissāmī’’ti mahāpaṭiñ‍ñā pavattitā| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘kiṃ mai aikaina tiṇṇaina, purisaina thāmadassinā|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇitvā, santāraissaṃ sadaivakaṃ||

‘‘iminā mai adhikāraina, kataina purisuttamai|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇitvā, tāraimi janataṃ bahuṃ||

‘‘saṃsārasautaṃ chinditvā, viddhaṃsaitvā tayau bhavai|

dhammanāvaṃ samāruyha, santāraissaṃ sadaivakaṃ||

‘‘kiṃ mai añ‍ñātavaisaina, dhammaṃ sacchikatainidha|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇitvā, buddhau haissaṃ sadaivakai’’ti|| (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.55-58)|

taṃ  panaitaṃ mahāpaṭiñ‍ñaṃ sakalassāpi buddhakaradhammasamudāyassa pavicaya pac‍cavaikkhaṇasamādānānaṃ kāraṇabhūtaṃ  avisaṃvādaitvā laukanāyakau yasmā mahākappānaṃ satasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni sak‍kac‍caṃ  nirantaraṃ niravasaisatau dānapāramiādayau samatiṃsapāramiyau pūraitvā, aṅgaparic‍cāgādayau pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai paric‍cajitvā, sac‍cādhiṭṭhānādīni cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni paribrūhaitvā, puñ‍ñañāṇasambhārai sambharitvā, pubbayaugapubbacariyadhammakkhānañātatthacariyādayau uk‍kaṃsāpaitvā, buddhicariyaṃ paramakauṭiṃ pāpaitvā, anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhi, tasmā tassaiva sā mahāpaṭiñ‍ñā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā, na tassa vālaggamattampi vitathaṃ atthi| tathā hi dīpaṅkaradasabalau kauṇḍañ‍ñau maṅgalau…pai॰… kassapau bhagavāti imai catuvīsati sammāsambuddhā paṭipāṭiyā uppan‍nā ‘‘buddhau bhavissatī’’ti byākariṃsu| aivaṃ catuvīsatiyā buddhānaṃ santikai laddhabyākaraṇau yai tai katābhinīhāraihi baudhisattaihi laddhabbā ānisaṃsā, tai labhitvāva āgatauti tāya yathāvuttāya mahāpaṭiñ‍ñāya tathāya abhisambuddhabhāvaṃ āgatau adhigatauti tathāgatau| aivaṃ tathāya āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathāya gatauti tathāgatau? yāya mahākaruṇāya laukanāthau mahādukkhasambādhappaṭipan‍naṃ sattanikāyaṃ disvā ‘‘tassa natthañ‍ñau kauci paṭissaraṇaṃ, ahamaiva namitau saṃsāradukkhatau muttau maucaissāmī’’ti samussāhitamānasau mahābhinīhāramakāsi| katvā ca yathāpaṇidhānaṃ sakalalaukahitasampādanāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍nau attanau kāyajīvitanirapaikkhau paraisaṃ sautapathāgamanamattainapi cittutrāsasamuppādikā atiduk‍karā duk‍karacariyā samācarantau yathā mahābaudhiyā paṭipatti hānabhāgiyā saṃkilaisabhāgiyā ṭhitibhāgiyā vā na hauti, atha khau uttaruttari visaisabhāgiyāva hauti, tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau anupubbaina niravasaisai baudhisambhārai sampādaitvā abhisambaudhiṃ pāpuṇi| tatau parañ‍ca tāyaiva mahākaruṇāya sañ‍cauditamānasau pavivaikaratiṃ paramañ‍ca santaṃ vimaukkhasukhaṃ pahāya bālajanabahulai laukai taihi samuppāditaṃ sammānāvamānavippakāraṃ agaṇaitvā vinaiyyajanassa vinayanaina niravasaisaṃ buddhakic‍caṃ  niṭṭhapaisi| tattha yau bhagavatau sattaisu mahākaruṇāya samauk‍kamanākārau, sau paratau āvibhavissati| yathā ca buddhabhūtassa  laukanāthassa sattaisu mahākaruṇā, aivaṃ baudhisattabhūtassapi mahābhinīhārakālādīsūti sabbattha sabbadā ca aikasadisatāya tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā, tasmā tīsupi avatthāsu sabbasattaisu samānarasāya tathāya mahākaruṇāya sakalalaukahitāya gatau paṭipan‍nauti tathāgatau| aivaṃ tathāya gatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau? tathāni nāma cattāri ariyamaggañāṇāni| tāni hi  idaṃ dukkhaṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti aivaṃ sabbañaiyyadhammasaṅgāhakānaṃ pavattinivattitadubhayahaitubhūtānaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau saṅkhataṭṭhau santāpaṭṭhau vipariṇāmaṭṭhau, samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau nidānaṭṭhau saṃyaugaṭṭhau palibaudhaṭṭhau, niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau vivaikaṭṭhau asaṅkhataṭṭhau amataṭṭhau, maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau haitvaṭṭhau dassanaṭṭhau ādhipataiyyaṭṭhautiādīnaṃ tabbibhāgānañ‍ca yathābhūtasabhāvāvabaudhavibandhakassa saṃkilaisapakkhassa samucchindanaina paṭiladdhāya tattha asammauhābhisamayasaṅkhātāya aviparītākārappavattiyā dhammānaṃ sabhāvasarasalakkhaṇassa avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni, tāni bhagavā anañ‍ñanaiyyau sayamaiva āgatau adhigatau, tasmā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

yathā ca maggañāṇāni, aivaṃ bhagavatau tīsu kālaisu appaṭihatañāṇāni catupaṭisambhidāñāṇāni catuvaisāraj‍jañāṇāni pañ‍cagatiparicchaidañāṇāni chaasādhāraṇañāṇāni sattabaujjhaṅgavibhāvanañāṇāni aṭṭhamaggaṅgavibhāvanañāṇāni navānupubbavihārasamāpattiñāṇāni dasabalañāṇāni ca tathabhāvai vaiditabbāni|

tatrāyaṃ vibhāvanā – yañhi kiñ‍ci aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ hīnādibhaidabhin‍nāsu atītāsu khandhāyatanadhātūsu sabhāvakic‍cādi  avatthāvisaisādi khandhappaṭibaddhanāmagauttādi ca jānitabbaṃ| anindriyabaddhaisu ca atisukhumatirauhitavidūradaisaisupi rūpadhammaisu yau taṃtaṃpac‍cayavisaisaihi saddhiṃ pac‍cayuppan‍nānaṃ vaṇṇasaṇṭhānagandharasaphassādivisaisau, tattha sabbatthaiva hatthatalai ṭhapitaāmalakai viya  pac‍cakkhatau appaṭihataṃ bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati, tathā anāgatāsu pac‍cuppan‍nāsu cāti imāni tīsu kālaisu appaṭihatañāṇāni nāma| yathāha –

‘‘atītaṃsai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, anāgataṃsai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍naṃsai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.5)|

tāni panaitāni tattha tattha dhammānaṃ sabhāvasarasalakkhaṇassa avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni, tāni bhagavā sayambhūñāṇaina adhigañchīti| aivampi tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā  atthappaṭisambhidā dhammappaṭisambhidā niruttippaṭisambhidā paṭibhānappaṭisambhidāti catassau paṭisambhidā| tattha atthappabhaidassa sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ atthappabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā| dhammappabhaidassa sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ dhammappabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā| niruttippabhaidassa sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ niruttābhilāpai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ niruttippaṭisambhidā| paṭibhānappabhaidassa sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ paṭibhānappabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānappaṭisambhidā| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘atthai ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā, dhammai ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttippaṭisambhidā, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānappaṭisambhidā’’ti (vibha॰ 718-721)|

aittha ca haituanusāraina araṇīyatau adhigantabbatau ca saṅkhaipatau haituphalaṃ atthau nāma| pabhaidatau pana yaṃ kiñ‍ci pac‍cayuppan‍naṃ, nibbānaṃ, bhāsitatthau, vipākau, kiriyāti imai pañ‍ca dhammā atthau| taṃ atthaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa tasmiṃ atthai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā| dhammauti saṅkhaipatau pac‍cayau| sau hi yasmā taṃ taṃ atthaṃ vidahati pavattaiti caiva pāpaiti ca, tasmā dhammauti vuc‍cati, pabhaidatau pana yau kauci phalanibbattanakau haitu ariyamaggau bhāsitaṃ kusalaṃ akusalanti imai pañ‍ca dhammā dhammau, taṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa tasmiṃ dhammai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘dukkhai  ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā’’ti (vibha॰ 719)|

atha vā haitumhi ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā, haituphalai ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā| yai dhammā jātā bhūtā sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā, imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā| yamhā dhammā tai dhammā jātā bhūtā sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā, taisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā| jarāmaraṇai ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā, jarāmaraṇasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā| jarāmaraṇaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā| jātiyā, bhavai, upādānai, taṇhāya, vaidanāya, phassai, saḷāyatanai, nāmarūpai, viñ‍ñāṇai, saṅkhāraisu ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā, saṅkhārasamudayai  ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā| saṅkhāraniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā|

‘‘idha bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānāti suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ…pai॰… vaidal‍laṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati dhammappaṭisambhidā| sau tassa tassaiva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ imassa  bhāsitassa atthau’ti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati atthappaṭisambhidā|

‘‘katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammappaṭisambhidā, taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthappaṭisambhidā’’tiādi (vibha॰ 724-725) vitthārau|

yā panaitasmiṃ atthai ca dhammai ca sabhāvanirutti abyabhicāravauhārau abhilāpau, tasmiṃ sabhāvaniruttābhilāpai māgadhikāya sabbasattānaṃ mūlabhāsāya ‘‘ayaṃ sabhāvanirutti, ayaṃ asabhāvaniruttī’’ti pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ niruttippaṭisambhidā| yathāvuttaisu taisu ñāṇaisu gaucarakic‍catau vitthāratau pavattaṃ sabbampi taṃ ñāṇaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhantassa tasmiṃ ñāṇai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānappaṭisambhidā| iti imāni cattāri  paṭisambhidāñāṇāni sayamaiva bhagavatā adhigatāni atthadhammādikai tasmiṃ tasmiṃ attanau visayai avisaṃvādanavasaina aviparītākārappavattiyā tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā yaṃkiñ‍ci ñaiyyaṃ nāma, sabbaṃ taṃ bhagavatā sabbākāraina ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ adhigataṃ abhisambuddhaṃ| tathā hissa abhiñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyatau buddhā, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyatau, pahātabbā dhammā pahātabbatau, sacchikātabbā dhammā sacchikātabbatau, bhāvaitabbā dhammā bhāvaitabbatau, yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā ‘‘imai nāma tai dhammā anabhisambuddhā’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthi|

yaṃkiñ‍ci pahātabbaṃ nāma, sabbaṃ taṃ bhagavatau anavasaisatau baudhimūlaiyaiva pahīnaṃ anuppattidhammaṃ, na tassa pahānāya uttari karaṇīyaṃ atthi| tathā hissa laubhadausamauhaviparītamanasikāraahirikānauttappathinamiddhakaudhūpanāha- makkhapalāsaissāmacchariyamāyāsāṭhaiyyathambhasārambhamānātimānamadappamādatividhā- kusalamūladuc‍caritavisamaviparītasañ‍ñā-  malavitak‍kapapañ‍caaisanātaṇhācatubbidhavipariyaisaāsavagantha- aughayaugāgatitaṇhupādānapañ‍cābhinandananīvaraṇacaitaukhilacaitasauvinibandha- chavivādamūlasattānusayaaṭṭhamicchattanavaāghātavatthutaṇhā- mūlakadasaakusalakammapathaaikavīsati anaisanadvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigataaṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritādippabhaidaṃ diyaḍḍhakilaisasahassaṃ saha vāsanāya pahīnaṃ samucchin‍naṃ samūhataṃ, yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā…pai॰… brahmā vā ‘‘imai nāma tai kilaisā appahīnā’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthi|

yai cimai bhagavatā kammavipākakilaisūpavādaāṇāvītik‍kamappabhaidā antarāyikā vuttā, alamaiva tai paṭisaivatau aikantaina antarāyāya| yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā…pai॰… brahmā vā ‘‘nālaṃ tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthi|

yau ca bhagavatā niravasaisavaṭṭadukkhanissaraṇāya sīlasamādhipañ‍ñāya saṅgahau sattakauṭṭhāsikau sattatiṃsappabhaidau ariyamaggapubbaṅgamau anuttarau  niyyānikau dhammau daisitau, sau aikantainaiva niyyāti, paṭipan‍nassa vaṭṭadukkhatau maukkhāya hauti, yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā…pai॰… brahmā vā ‘‘niyyānikau dhammauti tayā daisitau na niyyātī’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthīti| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘sammāsambuddhassa tai paṭijānatau imai dhammā anabhisambuddhā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.150) vitthārau| aivamaitāni attanau ñāṇappahānadaisanāvisaisānaṃ avitathabhāvāvabaudhanatau  aviparītākārappavattitāni bhagavatau catuvaisāraj‍jañāṇāni tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā nirayagati, tiracchānagati, paitagati, manussagati, daivagatīti pañ‍ca gatiyau, tāsu sañ‍jīvādayau aṭṭha mahānirayā, kuk‍kuḷādayau sauḷasa ussadanirayā, laukantarikanirayauti sabbaipimai aikantadukkhatāya nirassādaṭṭhaina nirayā, yathākammunā gantabbatau gati cāti nirayagati, tibbandhakārasītanarakāpi aitaisvaiva antaugadhā| kimikīṭasarīsapapakkhisauṇasiṅgālādayau tiriyaṃ añchitabhāvaina tiracchānā, tai aiva gatīti tiracchānagati| khuppipāsitattā paradattūpajīvinijjhāmataṇhikādayau dukkhabahulatāya pākaṭasukhatau  itā vigatāti paitā, tai aiva gatīti paitagati, kālakañ‍cikādiasurāpi aitaisvaivantaugadhā| parittadīpavāsīhi saddhiṃ jambudīpādicatumahādīpavāsinau manasau ussan‍natāya manussā, tai aiva gatīti manussagati| cātumahārājikatau paṭṭhāya yāva naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagāti imai chabbīsati daivanikāyā dibbanti attanau iddhānubhāvaina kīḷanti jautanti cāti daivā, tai aiva gatīti daivagati|

tā panaitā gatiyau yasmā taṃtaṃkammanibbattau upapattibhavavisaisau, tasmā atthatau vipākakkhandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| tattha ‘‘ayaṃ nāma gati iminā nāma kammunā jāyati, tassa ca kammassa pac‍cayavisaisaihi aivaṃ vibhāgabhin‍nattā visuṃ aitai sattanikāyā aivaṃ vibhāgabhin‍nā’’ti yathāsakaṃ haituphalavibhāgaparicchindanavasaina ṭhānasau haitusau bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati| taināha bhagavā –

‘‘pañ‍ca  khau imā, sāriputta, gatiyau| katamā pañ‍ca? nirayau, tiracchānayauni, paittivisayau, manussā, daivā| nirayañ‍cāhaṃ, sāriputta, pajānāmi nirayagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ nirayagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ, yathā paṭipan‍nau ca kāyassa bhaidā  parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tañ‍ca pajānāmī’’tiādi (ma॰ ni॰ 1.153)|

tāni panaitāni bhagavatau ñāṇāni tasmiṃ tasmiṃ visayai aviparītākārappavattiyā avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā yaṃ sattānaṃ saddhādiyaugavikalāvikalabhāvāvabaudhanaina apparajakkhamahārajakkhatādivisaisavibhāvanaṃ pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi pavattaṃ bhagavatau indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘saddhau puggalau apparajakkhau, assaddhau puggalau mahārajakkhau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.111) vitthārau|

yañ‍ca ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau apparajakkhau, ayaṃ sassatadiṭṭhikau, ayaṃ ucchaidadiṭṭhikau, ayaṃ anulaumikāyaṃ khantiyaṃ ṭhitau, ayaṃ yathābhūtañāṇai ṭhitau, ayaṃ kāmāsayau, na naikkhammādiāsayau, ayaṃ naikkhammāsayau, na kāmādiāsayau’’tiādinā ‘‘imassa kāmarāgau ativiya  thāmagatau, na paṭighādikau, imassa paṭighau ativiya thāmagatau, na kāmarāgādikau’’tiādinā ‘‘imassa puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau adhikau, na apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau na ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau, imassa apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau adhikau, na puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau na ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau, imassa ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau adhikau, na puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau na apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau| imassa kāyasucaritaṃ adhikaṃ, imassa vacīsucaritaṃ, imassa manausucaritaṃ, ayaṃ hīnādhimuttikau, ayaṃ paṇītādhimuttikau, ayaṃ kammāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, ayaṃ kilaisāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, ayaṃ vipākāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, ayaṃ na kammāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, na kilaisāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, na vipākāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau’’tiādinā ca sattānaṃ āsayādīnaṃ yathābhūtaṃ vibhāvanākārappavattaṃ bhagavatau āsayānusayañāṇaṃ| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘idha  tathāgatau sattānaṃ āsayaṃ jānāti, anusayaṃ jānāti, caritaṃ jānāti, adhimuttiṃ jānāti, bhabbābhabbai sattai jānātī’’tiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.113)|

yañ‍ca uparimahaiṭṭhimapurimapacchimakāyaihi dakkhiṇavāmaakkhikaṇṇasautanāsikasautaaṃsakūṭahatthapādaihi aṅguliaṅgulantaraihi laumakūpaihi ca aggikkhandhūdakadhārāpavattanaṃ anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaṃ vividhavikubbaniddhinimmāpanakaṃ  bhagavatau yamakapāṭihāriyañāṇaṃ| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘idha tathāgatau yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ karauti asādhāraṇaṃ sāvakaihi, uparimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, haiṭṭhimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati| haiṭṭhimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, uparimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattatī’’tiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.116)|

yañ‍ca rāgādīhi jātiādīhi ca anaikaihi dukkhadhammaihi upaddutaṃ sattanikāyaṃ tatau nīharitukāmatāvasaina nānānayaihi pavattassa bhagavatau mahākaruṇauk‍kamanassa pac‍cayabhūtaṃ mahākaruṇāsamāpattiñāṇaṃ| yathāha (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.117-118) –
